```javascript
// ============================================================================
// File: functions/api/worker.js (johntjz/Hk-Fleet — Self-Contained ESM Worker)
// Patch: 2026-11-20 20:28 HKT — Keep Full 4-Geocoder + SF-H + CSV Parser; Add 8 HK Corridors + HKO Weather
// ============================================================================

const CORS_HEADERS = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type',
  'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8'
};

const CORRIDOR_PATTERNS = {
  LANTAU_TM:    /tuen\s*mun.*chek\s*lap\s*kok|tm-?clk|north\s*lantau|lantau\s*link|tsing\s*ma|kap\s*shui\s*mun|south\s*lantau\s*road|tung\s*chung\s*road|penny'?s\s*bay/i,
  TUEN_MUN_RD:  /tuen\s*mun\s*road|ting\s*kau\s*bridge|ting\s*kau/i,
  TAI_LAM:      /tai\s*lam\s*tunnel|tsing\s*long\s*highway|yuen\s*long\s*highway/i,
  TOLO_HWY:     /tolo\s*highway|fanling\s*highway|tai\s*po\s*road/i,
  LION_TATES:   /lion\s*rock\s*tunnel|tate'?s\s*cairn|tai\s*lo\s*shan/i,
  SHING_MUN:    /shing\s*mun\s*tunnel|tsing\s*sha\s*highway|eagle'?s\s*nest|sha\s*tin\s*heights/i,
  HARBOUR_TUNS: /cross[\s-]*harbour\s*tunnel|western\s*harbour|eastern\s*harbour|central[\s-]*wan\s*chai\s*bypass/i,
  TKO_TUN:      /tseung\s*kwan\s*o\s*tunnel|tseung\s*kwan\s*o[\s-]*lam\s*tin|cross\s*bay\s*link/i
};

function normKey(s, stripPierSuffix = true) {
  let out = String(s || '').toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s*[\(（][^\)）]*[\)）]\s*/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+at\s+.*$/i, '')
    .split(',')[0]
    .replace(/\bcentral\s*(?:ferry\s*)?pier\s*(?:no\.?\s*)?\d+\b/gi, 'central')
    .replace(/\b(bus terminus|public transport interchange|pti|bbi|mtr station|light rail stop|light rail|mtr|railway station|train station|station|stop|halt)\b/gi, '')
    .replace(/(巴士總站|公共運輸交匯處|轉車站|港鐵站|輕鐵站|鐵路站|輕鐵|港鐵|車站|站)/g, '');
  if (stripPierSuffix) {
    out = out
      .replace(/\b(ferry pier|public pier|ferry terminal|promenade landing steps|landing steps|new development ferry pier|pier\s*no\.?\s*\d+|pier)\b/gi, '')
      .replace(/(新發展碼頭|海濱長廊登岸梯級|渡輪碼頭|公眾碼頭|客運碼頭|登岸梯級|碼頭|[一二三四五六七八九十\d]+號碼頭)/g, '');
  }
  return out.replace(/[^a-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fa5]/g, '');
}

async function fetchWithTimeout(url, timeoutMs = 4500, customHeaders = {}, cacheTtl = 60, method = 'GET', body = null) {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const tid = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const opts = {
      method,
      signal: ctrl.signal,
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) Transit-HK/4.0', ...customHeaders },
      cf: { cacheTtl, cacheEverything: true }
    };
    if (body) opts.body = body;
    const r = await fetch(url, opts);
    clearTimeout(tid);
    return r;
  } catch (e) {
    clearTimeout(tid);
    return null;
  }
}

function isValidHkCoord(lat, lon) {
  const la = parseFloat(lat), lo = parseFloat(lon);
  return Number.isFinite(la) && Number.isFinite(lo) && la > 22.15 && la < 22.58 && lo > 113.82 && lo < 114.45;
}

function parseCsv(text) {
  const lines = String(text || '').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '').split(/\r?\n/).filter(l => l.trim());
  if (!lines.length) return [];
  const splitRow = (row) => {
    const out = [];
    let cur = '', inQuotes = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < row.length; i++) {
      const c = row[i];
      if (c === '"') {
        if (inQuotes && row[i + 1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; }
        else inQuotes = !inQuotes;
      } else if (c === ',' && !inQuotes) {
        out.push(cur.trim()); cur = '';
      } else cur += c;
    }
    out.push(cur.trim());
    return out;
  };
  const headers = splitRow(lines[0]);
  return lines.slice(1).map(l => {
    const vals = splitRow(l);
    const obj = {};
    headers.forEach((h, i) => obj[h] = vals[i] || '');
    return obj;
  });
}

function parseXmlRecords(xmlText, rowTag) {
  const out = [];
  const rowRegex = new RegExp(`<${rowTag}>([\\s\\S]*?)<\\/${rowTag}>`, 'gi');
  const tagRegex = /<([A-Z0-9_]+)>([\s\S]*?)<\/\1>/gi;
  let m;
  while ((m = rowRegex.exec(String(xmlText || ''))) !== null) {
    const obj = {};
    let tm;
    tagRegex.lastIndex = 0;
    while ((tm = tagRegex.exec(m[1])) !== null) {
      obj[tm[1]] = tm[2]
        .replace(/<!\[CDATA\[([\s\S]*?)\]\]>/g, '$1')
        .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
        .replace(/&apos;/g, "'")
        .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
        .trim();
    }
    out.push(obj);
  }
  return out;
}

function hk80ToWgs84LocalFallback(north, east) {
  const n = Number(north), e = Number(east);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n) || !Number.isFinite(e) || n < 790000 || e < 790000) return null;
  const lat = 22.3121333 + (n - 819069.80) / 110730.0 - 0.0015278;
  const lon = 114.1785556 + (e - 836694.05) / 102990.0 + 0.0024444;
  if (isValidHkCoord(lat, lon)) {
    return { lat: Number(lat.toFixed(6)), lon: Number(lon.toFixed(6)) };
  }
  return null;
}

async function hk80ToWgs84Api(north, east, allowNetwork = true) {
  if (allowNetwork) {
    try {
      const r = await fetchWithTimeout(
        `https://www.geodetic.gov.hk/transform/v2/?inSys=hkgrid&outSys=wgsgeog&n=${encodeURIComponent(north)}&e=${encodeURIComponent(east)}`,
        2200,
        { 'Accept': 'application/json' },
        86400
      );
      if (r && r.ok) {
        const d = await r.json().catch(() => null);
        const lat = parseFloat(d?.wgsLat), lon = parseFloat(d?.wgsLong);
        if (isValidHkCoord(lat, lon)) {
          return { lat: Number(lat.toFixed(6)), lon: Number(lon.toFixed(6)) };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return hk80ToWgs84LocalFallback(north, east);
}

// Operator Ferry CSV Schedule Parser (Only fetched when explicitly requested via ?sched=1)
async function fetchOperatorFerryCsvSchedules() {
  const ferrySchedByPair = new Map();
  const hktTodayYmd = new Date(Date.now() + 8 * 3600000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);

  const addSample = (pairKey, isWk, isSat, isHol, depMin, slotDur = 0, updatedStr = '') => {
    if (!pairKey || !Number.isFinite(depMin) || depMin < 0 || depMin >= 1440) return;
    if (!ferrySchedByPair.has(pairKey)) {
      ferrySchedByPair.set(pairKey, { wk: new Map(), sat: new Map(), hol: new Map(), updated: '' });
    }
    const b = ferrySchedByPair.get(pairKey);
    if (updatedStr && !b.updated) b.updated = updatedStr;
    if (isWk) b.wk.set(depMin, slotDur || b.wk.get(depMin) || 0);
    if (isSat) b.sat.set(depMin, slotDur || b.sat.get(depMin) || 0);
    if (isHol) b.hol.set(depMin, slotDur || b.hol.get(depMin) || 0);
  };

  const feeds = [
    { pair: ['central_cheungchau', 'cheungchau_central'], url: 'https://www.sunferry.com.hk/eta/timetable/SunFerry_central_cheungchau_timetable_eng.csv', isOutlyingDualSpeed: true },
    { pair: ['central_muiwo', 'muiwo_central'], url: 'https://www.sunferry.com.hk/eta/timetable/SunFerry_central_muiwo_timetable_eng.csv', isOutlyingDualSpeed: true },
    { pair: ['northpoint_hunghom', 'hunghom_northpoint'], url: 'https://www.sunferry.com.hk/eta/timetable/SunFerry_northpoint_hunghom_timetable_eng.csv' },
    { pair: ['northpoint_kowlooncity', 'kowlooncity_northpoint'], url: 'https://www.sunferry.com.hk/eta/timetable/SunFerry_northpoint_kowlooncity_timetable_eng.csv' },
    { pair: ['pengchau_cheungchau', 'cheungchau_pengchau'], url: 'https://www.sunferry.com.hk/eta/timetable/SunFerry_inter_islands_timetable_eng.csv' },
    { pair: ['central_tsimshatsui', 'tsimshatsui_central'], url: 'https://www.starferry.com.hk/sites/default/files/upload/open_data/csv/ferry_sf_central_tsimshatsui_timetable_eng.csv' },
    { pair: ['wanchai_tsimshatsui', 'tsimshatsui_wanchai'], url: 'https://www.starferry.com.hk/sites/default/files/upload/open_data/csv/ferry_sf_wanchai_tsimshatsui_timetable_eng.csv' },
    { pair: ['central_hunghom', 'hunghom_central'], url: 'https://www.hongkongwatertaxi.com.hk/csv/CHHTimetable_eng.csv' },
    { pair: ['northpoint_kwuntong', 'kwuntong_northpoint'], url: 'https://www.hongkongwatertaxi.com.hk/csv/NPKTTimetable_eng.csv' },
    { pair: ['tuenmun_taio', 'taio_tuenmun'], url: 'https://www.hongkongwatertaxi.com.hk/csv/TMTOTimetable_eng.csv' }
  ];

  await Promise.allSettled(feeds.map(async (feed) => {
    const res = await fetchWithTimeout(feed.url, 3500, {}, 1800);
    if (!res || !res.ok) return;
    const lm = res.headers.get('last-modified');
    const rawText = await res.text();
    const updDate = (lm && !isNaN(Date.parse(lm)))
      ? new Date(lm).toISOString().slice(0, 10)
      : (rawText.match(/20\d{2}[-/]\d{1,2}[-/]\d{1,2}/)?.[0] || hktTodayYmd);

    const rows = parseCsv(rawText);
    const [p0a, p0b] = feed.pair[0].split('_');
    let dayMask = { wk: true, sat: false, hol: false };
    const lastMinByBucket = new Map();

    const parseCellMinutes = (str, bucketSig) => {
      const out = [];
      const re = /\b([01]?\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)(?:\s*(a\.?m\.?|p\.?m\.?|nn|noon|mn|midnight))?/gi;
      let m;
      while ((m = re.exec(String(str || ''))) !== null) {
        let h = Number(m[1]), mn = Number(m[2]);
        const suf = (m[3] || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\./g, '');
        if (suf.startsWith('p')) h = (h % 12) + 12;
        else if (suf.startsWith('a') || suf === 'mn' || suf === 'midnight') h = h % 12;
        else if (suf === 'nn' || suf === 'noon') h = 12;
        else if (h >= 1 && h <= 11 && !/^0\d:/.test(m[0])) {
          const prev = lastMinByBucket.get(bucketSig) ?? -1;
          if (prev >= 690 && (h * 60 + mn) < prev - 180) h += 12;
        }
        const totalMin = (h % 24) * 60 + mn;
        lastMinByBucket.set(bucketSig, totalMin);
        out.push(totalMin);
      }
      return out;
    };

    rows.forEach(row => {
      const entries = Object.entries(row);
      const rowStr = entries.map(([, v]) => v).join(' ');
      const dayCandidate = entries
        .filter(([k]) => /day|date|service|星期|日子/i.test(k) && !/hour|time/i.test(k))
        .map(([, v]) => v).join(' ') || String(entries[0]?.[1] || '');

      if (/monday|weekday|saturday|sunday|holiday|daily|星期|平日|假日|公眾假期/i.test(dayCandidate)) {
        const cleanDay = String(dayCandidate)
          .replace(/[\(（]?except\s*public\s*holidays?[\)）]?/gi, ' ')
          .replace(/[\(（]?公眾假期除外[\)）]?/g, ' ');
        const hol = /sunday|public\s*holiday|holiday|星期日|公眾假期|假日/i.test(cleanDay);
        const sat = /saturday|mon(?:day)?s?\s*(?:to|-)\s*sat(?:urday)?s?|daily|星期六|每日/i.test(cleanDay);
        const wk  = (/monday|weekday|daily|星期一|平日|每日/i.test(cleanDay) && !/saturday\s*only/i.test(cleanDay)) || (!hol && !sat);
        dayMask = { wk, sat, hol };
      }

      const daySig = `${dayMask.wk ? 1 : 0}${dayMask.sat ? 1 : 0}${dayMask.hol ? 1 : 0}`;
      const rmkCells = entries.filter(([k]) => /remark|type|vessel|class|備註|船種/i.test(k)).map(([, v]) => String(v || '').trim()).join(' ');
      let slotDur = 0;
      if (/fast\s*ferry|高速/i.test(rowStr)) slotDur = 38;
      else if (/ordinary\s*ferry|普通/i.test(rowStr)) slotDur = 55;
      else if (feed.isOutlyingDualSpeed) {
        if (/\b0\b/.test(rmkCells)) slotDur = 55;
        else if (/\b1\b/.test(rmkCells)) slotDur = 38;
      }

      if (feed.url.includes('inter_islands')) {
        const pierTimes = {};
        for (const [colHdr, cellVal] of entries) {
          const hClean = colHdr.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, '');
          const p = ['pengchau', 'muiwo', 'chimawan', 'cheungchau'].find(x => hClean.includes(x));
          if (!p || !cellVal) continue;
          const mins = parseCellMinutes(cellVal, `ii_${p}_${daySig}`);
          if (mins.length) pierTimes[p] = mins[0];
        }
        const order = ['pengchau', 'muiwo', 'chimawan', 'cheungchau'].filter(p => pierTimes[p] !== undefined);
        if (order.length >= 2) {
          const diff = (pierTimes[order[order.length - 1]] - pierTimes[order[0]] + 1440) % 1440;
          const seq = (diff > 0 && diff < 180) ? order : [...order].reverse();
          for (let i = 0; i < seq.length - 1; i++) {
            for (let j = i + 1; j < seq.length; j++) {
              addSample(`${seq[i]}_${seq[j]}`, dayMask.wk, dayMask.sat, dayMask.hol, pierTimes[seq[i]], (j - i) * 18, updDate);
            }
          }
        }
        return;
      }

      let matchedCols = false;
      if (!feed.url.includes('starferry')) {
        for (const [colHdr, cellVal] of entries) {
          if (!cellVal) continue;
          const hClean = colHdr.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, '');
          if (/origin|destination|route|direction|remark|day|date/i.test(hClean) && !/from|depart|time|hour/.test(hClean)) continue;
          const idxA = hClean.indexOf(p0a), idxB = hClean.indexOf(p0b);
          const colPair = (idxA !== -1 && idxB !== -1) ? (idxA < idxB ? feed.pair[0] : feed.pair[1])
            : (/from|depart/.test(hClean) ? (idxA !== -1 ? feed.pair[0] : (idxB !== -1 ? feed.pair[1] : null)) : null);
          if (!colPair) continue;
          const cellDur = slotDur || (feed.isOutlyingDualSpeed && /\*/.test(cellVal) ? 55 : (feed.isOutlyingDualSpeed ? 38 : 0));
          const mins = parseCellMinutes(cellVal, `${colPair}_${daySig}`);
          if (mins.length) {
            matchedCols = true;
            mins.forEach(mn => addSample(colPair, dayMask.wk, dayMask.sat, dayMask.hol, mn, cellDur, updDate));
          }
        }
      }
      if (matchedCols) return;

      const origCol = normKey(row.Origin || row.From || '', true), destCol = normKey(row.Destination || row.To || '', true);
      let pKey = (origCol.includes(p0a) || destCol.includes(p0b)) ? feed.pair[0]
        : ((origCol.includes(p0b) || destCol.includes(p0a)) ? feed.pair[1] : null);
      if (!pKey) {
        const dc = String(row.Direction || row.Route || row.Service || rowStr).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, '');
        const i0 = dc.indexOf(p0a), i1 = dc.indexOf(p0b);
        pKey = (i0 !== -1 && (i1 === -1 || i0 < i1)) ? feed.pair[0] : feed.pair[1];
      }
      const timeCells = entries.filter(([k]) => !/day|date|route|origin|dest|direction|remark/i.test(k) || /hour|time/i.test(k)).map(([, v]) => v).join(' ');
      const mins = parseCellMinutes(timeCells || rowStr, `${pKey}_${daySig}`);

      if (feed.url.includes('starferry') && mins.length >= 2) {
        const sM = mins[0]; let eM = mins[1]; if (eM <= sM) eM += 1440;
        const step = Number((rowStr.replace(/\b([01]?\d|2[0-3]):([0-5]\d)(?:\s*[ap]\.?m\.?)?/gi, ' ').match(/\b(\d{1,2})\b/) || [])[1] || 0);
        if (step >= 4 && step <= 30) {
          for (let t = sM; t <= eM; t += step) addSample(pKey, dayMask.wk, dayMask.sat, dayMask.hol, t % 1440, 9, updDate);
        }
      } else {
        mins.forEach(mn => addSample(pKey, dayMask.wk, dayMask.sat, dayMask.hol, mn, slotDur, updDate));
      }
    });
  }));

  const out = {};
  const packMap = (m) => [...m.entries()].sort((a, c) => a[0] - c[0]).map(([min, d]) => d ? [min, d] : min);
  for (const [k, b] of ferrySchedByPair.entries()) {
    out[k] = {
      wk: packMap(b.wk),
      sat: packMap(b.sat),
      hol: packMap(b.hol),
      updated: b.updated || hktTodayYmd
    };
  }
  return out;
}

// 1. SCOPED LIVE FERRY PROXY (Includes Sun Ferry H-suffix Fast Ferry merge)
async function handleFerryLiveRequest(urlObj) {
  const reqKey = (urlObj?.searchParams?.get('key') || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const liveEtas = {};

  const sfGroups = {
    SF_CECC: ['CECC', 'CECCH'], SF_CCCE: ['CCCE', 'CCCEH'],
    SF_CEMW: ['CEMW', 'CEMWH'], SF_MWCE: ['MWCE', 'MWCEH'],
    SF_NPHH: ['NPHH'],          SF_HHNP: ['HHNP'],
    SF_NPKC: ['NPKC'],          SF_KCNP: ['KCNP'],
    SF_PCMW: ['PCMW'],          SF_MWPC: ['MWPC'],
    SF_CCMW: ['CCMW'],          SF_MWCC: ['MWCC']
  };

  const tasks = [];

  if (reqKey !== '_NONE') {
    for (const [targetKey, codes] of Object.entries(sfGroups)) {
      if (reqKey && reqKey !== targetKey) continue;
      tasks.push((async () => {
        const merged = [];
        await Promise.all(codes.map(async (rc) => {
          const r = await fetchWithTimeout(`https://www.sunferry.com.hk/eta/?route=${encodeURIComponent(rc)}`, 3200, {}, 20);
          if (r && r.ok) {
            const d = await r.json().catch(() => null);
            const rows = Array.isArray(d?.data) ? d.data : [];
            rows.forEach(row => {
              const rowTxt = Object.values(row || {}).join(' ');
              const isFast = rc.endsWith('H') || /fast|高速/i.test(rowTxt);
              const isOrd  = !isFast && /ordinary|普通/i.test(rowTxt);
              merged.push({
                ...row,
                vessel_type: isFast ? 'Fast Ferry' : (isOrd ? 'Ordinary Ferry' : (row.vessel_type || '')),
                vessel_type_tc: isFast ? '高速船' : (isOrd ? '普通渡輪' : (row.vessel_type_tc || ''))
              });
            });
          }
        }));
        if (merged.length > 0) liveEtas[targetKey] = merged;
      })());
    }

    for (let routeId = 1; routeId <= 4; routeId++) {
      for (const dir of ['inbound', 'outbound']) {
        const k = `HKKF_${routeId}_${dir === 'outbound' ? 'O' : 'I'}`;
        if (reqKey && reqKey !== k) continue;
        tasks.push((async () => {
          const r = await fetchWithTimeout(`https://www.hkkfeta.com/opendata/eta/${routeId}/${dir}`, 3200, {}, 20);
          if (r && r.ok) {
            const d = await r.json().catch(() => null);
            if (Array.isArray(d?.data) && d.data.length > 0) liveEtas[k] = d.data;
          }
        })());
      }
    }

    for (const ffCode of ['CHH', 'HHC', 'NPKT', 'KTNP', 'TMTO', 'TOTM']) {
      const k = `FF_${ffCode}`;
      if (reqKey && reqKey !== k) continue;
      tasks.push((async () => {
        const r = await fetchWithTimeout(`https://www.hongkongwatertaxi.com.hk/eta/?route=${encodeURIComponent(ffCode)}`, 3200, {}, 20);
        if (r && r.ok) {
          const d = await r.json().catch(() => null);
          if (Array.isArray(d?.data) && d.data.length > 0) liveEtas[k] = d.data;
        }
      })());
    }
  }

  let csvSchedules = {};
  if (!reqKey || urlObj?.searchParams?.get('sched') === '1') {
    tasks.push((async () => {
      csvSchedules = await fetchOperatorFerryCsvSchedules();
    })());
  }

  await Promise.allSettled(tasks);
  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, ts: Date.now(), liveEtas, csvSchedules }), {
    headers: { ...CORS_HEADERS, 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=15' }
  });
}

// 2. DOMESTIC HK GEOCODER (ALS + GeoData + Photon + Nominatim)
async function handleAddressLookup(rawQuery) {
  const q = String(rawQuery || '').trim();
  if (q.length < 1) return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results: [] }), { headers: CORS_HEADERS });

  const cleanedQ = q
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\b(limited|ltd|co\.?|inc\.?|corp\.?)\b/gi, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim() || q;

  const alsUrl = `https://www.als.gov.hk/lookup?q=${encodeURIComponent(cleanedQ)}&n=10&t=35`;
  const geoUrl = `https://geodata.gov.hk/gs/api/v1.0.0/locationSearch?q=${encodeURIComponent(cleanedQ)}`;
  const photonUrl = `https://photon.komoot.io/api/?q=${encodeURIComponent(cleanedQ)}&bbox=113.82,22.15,114.45,22.57&limit=6`;
  const nominatimUrl = `https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(cleanedQ)}&format=jsonv2&viewbox=113.82,22.57,114.45,22.15&bounded=1&addressdetails=1&namedetails=1&limit=5`;

  const [alsRes, geoRes, photonRes, nomRes] = await Promise.all([
    fetchWithTimeout(alsUrl, 2600, { 'Accept': 'application/json', 'Accept-Language': 'en,zh-Hant' }, 600),
    fetchWithTimeout(geoUrl, 2300, { 'Accept': 'application/json' }, 600),
    fetchWithTimeout(photonUrl, 2400, { 'Accept': 'application/json' }, 3600),
    fetchWithTimeout(nominatimUrl, 2500, { 'Accept': 'application/json', 'Accept-Language': 'en,zh-HK' }, 3600)
  ]);

  const toTitleCaseEn = (str) => String(str || '')
    .replace(/^(BLOCK|TOWER|FLAT|ROOM|UNIT|FLOOR|PHASE)\s+[A-Z0-9-]+\s*,\s*/i, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\b[a-z]/g, ch => ch.toUpperCase())
    .replace(/\bRoad\b/g, 'Rd')
    .replace(/\bStreet\b/g, 'St')
    .replace(/\bAvenue\b/g, 'Ave')
    .trim();

  const cleanZhPrefix = (str) => String(str || '')
    .replace(/^(第?[A-Z0-9一二三四五六七八九十]+(座|期|樓|室))\s*/i, '')
    .trim();

  const distMeters = (la1, lo1, la2, lo2) => Math.hypot((la2 - la1) * 111320, (lo2 - lo1) * 102900);

  const results = [];
  const pushUnique = (item) => {
    if (!isValidHkCoord(item.lat, item.lon)) return;
    const isDup = results.some(ex =>
      distMeters(ex.lat, ex.lon, item.lat, item.lon) < 95 &&
      (ex.name_en.toLowerCase() === item.name_en.toLowerCase() || (item.streetKey && ex.streetKey === item.streetKey))
    );
    if (!isDup && results.length < 10) results.push(item);
  };

  if (alsRes && alsRes.ok) {
    try {
      const data = await alsRes.json();
      const rawList = Array.isArray(data?.SuggestedAddress) ? data.SuggestedAddress : (data?.SuggestedAddress ? [data.SuggestedAddress] : []);
      for (const item of rawList) {
        const prem = item?.Address?.PremisesAddress;
        const geoRaw = prem?.GeospatialInformation;
        const geo = Array.isArray(geoRaw) ? geoRaw[0] : geoRaw;
        const lat = parseFloat(geo?.Latitude), lon = parseFloat(geo?.Longitude);
        const eng = prem?.EngPremisesAddress || {};
        const chi = prem?.ChiPremisesAddress || {};
        const engSt = [eng.EngStreet?.BuildingNoFrom, eng.EngStreet?.StreetName].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
        const chiSt = [chi.ChiStreet?.StreetName, chi.ChiStreet?.BuildingNoFrom ? `${chi.ChiStreet.BuildingNoFrom}號` : ''].filter(Boolean).join('');
        const rawEn = [eng.BuildingName, eng.EngEstate?.EstateName, engSt].filter(Boolean).join(', ') || cleanedQ;
        const name_en = toTitleCaseEn(rawEn);
        const chiBldg = cleanZhPrefix(chi.BuildingName || '');
        const name_zh = [chi.ChiEstate?.EstateName, chiBldg, chiSt].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim() || name_en;
        pushUnique({
          name_en,
          name_zh,
          name_tc: name_zh,
          district_en: toTitleCaseEn((eng.EngDistrict?.DcDistrict || '').replace(/\s+DISTRICT$/i, '')),
          district_zh: chi.ChiDistrict?.DcDistrict || '',
          streetKey: (engSt || name_en).toLowerCase(),
          lat: Number(lat.toFixed(6)),
          lon: Number(lon.toFixed(6))
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  if (geoRes && geoRes.ok) {
    try {
      const gList = await geoRes.json();
      if (Array.isArray(gList)) {
        const converted = await Promise.all(
          gList.slice(0, 6).map(async (g) => {
            if (!g?.x || !g?.y) return null;
            const wgs = await hk80ToWgs84Api(Number(g.y), Number(g.x), true);
            return wgs ? { g, wgs } : null;
          })
        );
        for (const item of converted) {
          if (!item) continue;
          const { g, wgs } = item;
          const name_en = toTitleCaseEn(g.nameEN || g.addressEN || cleanedQ);
          const name_zh = (g.nameZH || g.addressZH || name_en).trim();
          pushUnique({
            name_en,
            name_zh,
            name_tc: name_zh,
            district_en: toTitleCaseEn(g.districtEN || ''),
            district_zh: g.districtZH || '',
            streetKey: name_en.toLowerCase(),
            lat: Number(wgs.lat.toFixed(6)),
            lon: Number(wgs.lon.toFixed(6))
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  if (nomRes && nomRes.ok) {
    try {
      const nomList = await nomRes.json();
      if (Array.isArray(nomList)) {
        for (const n of nomList) {
          const lat = parseFloat(n.lat), lon = parseFloat(n.lon);
          const nameEn = n.namedetails?.['name:en'] || n.name || (n.display_name || '').split(',')[0];
          const nameZh = n.namedetails?.['name:zh-Hant'] || n.namedetails?.['name:zh'] || n.namedetails?.name || nameEn;
          const distEn = n.address?.suburb || n.address?.town || n.address?.city_district || n.address?.city || '';
          pushUnique({
            name_en: nameEn,
            name_zh: nameZh,
            name_tc: nameZh,
            district_en: distEn,
            district_zh: distEn,
            streetKey: nameEn.toLowerCase(),
            lat: Number(lat.toFixed(6)),
            lon: Number(lon.toFixed(6))
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  if (photonRes && photonRes.ok) {
    try {
      const pJson = await photonRes.json();
      for (const feat of (pJson?.features || [])) {
        const coords = feat?.geometry?.coordinates;
        const prop = feat?.properties || {};
        if (!Array.isArray(coords) || coords.length < 2 || !prop.name) continue;
        const lon = Number(coords[0]), lat = Number(coords[1]);
        const streetPart = [prop.housenumber, prop.street].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
        const rawLabel = [prop.name, streetPart].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
        pushUnique({
          name_en: rawLabel,
          name_zh: prop.name,
          name_tc: prop.name,
          district_en: prop.district || prop.city || '',
          district_zh: prop.district || '',
          streetKey: rawLabel.toLowerCase(),
          lat: Number(lat.toFixed(6)),
          lon: Number(lon.toFixed(6))
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, results }), { headers: CORS_HEADERS });
}

// 3. ALL 8 HK STRATEGIC CORRIDORS + HKO SEVERE WEATHER (Preserves partial-lane noise filter)
async function syncTdStrategicTrafficCorridorsAndWeather() {
  const highwayCorridors = {};
  for (const id of Object.keys(CORRIDOR_PATTERNS)) {
    highwayCorridors[id] = { id, speedKmh: 70, status: 'SMOOTH', headline: '' };
  }

  const weather = {
    heavyRain: false,
    rainAdvisory: false,
    typhoon: false,
    warningCodes: [],
    labelEn: '',
    labelTc: ''
  };

  const [stnRes, hkoRes] = await Promise.all([
    fetchWithTimeout('https://resource.data.one.gov.hk/td/en/specialtrafficnews.xml', 2800, {}, 60),
    fetchWithTimeout('https://data.weather.gov.hk/weatherAPI/opendata/weather.php?dataType=warnsum&lang=en', 2500, {}, 120)
  ]);

  try {
    const messages = stnRes?.ok ? parseXmlRecords(await stnRes.text(), 'message') : [];
    for (const msg of messages) {
      const curStatus = String(msg.CurrentStatus || '').toLowerCase();
      if (/cancel|clear|normal/.test(curStatus)) continue;

      const rawBody = `${msg.EngText || ''} ${msg.EngShort || ''}`.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      const body = rawBody.toLowerCase();
      if (!body || /re-?opened|back to normal|resumed|normal traffic|has been removed|disperse/.test(body)) continue;

      // Preserve strict partial-lane / routine roadworks noise filter
      const isPartialLaneOrWorks = /part of the lanes|one lane|remaining lane|slow lane|fast lane|middle lane|watermain|roadworks/i.test(body);
      const isFullBlockage = !isPartialLaneOrWorks && /all lanes.*closed|entire.*closed|full.*closure|bus service.*suspended|route.*suspended|traffic\s*accident|flooding|landslip/i.test(body);
      const isHeavyCongestion = !isPartialLaneOrWorks && /very\s*busy|congested|congestion|heavy\s*traffic|long\s*queue|seriously\s*delayed/i.test(body);
      if (!isFullBlockage && !isHeavyCongestion) continue;

      for (const [corId, pat] of Object.entries(CORRIDOR_PATTERNS)) {
        if (!pat.test(body)) continue;
        if (isFullBlockage) {
          highwayCorridors[corId] = {
            id: corId,
            speedKmh: 32,
            status: 'INCIDENT',
            headline: rawBody.slice(0, 140)
          };
        } else if (highwayCorridors[corId].status === 'SMOOTH') {
          highwayCorridors[corId] = {
            id: corId,
            speedKmh: 45,
            status: 'SLOW',
            headline: rawBody.slice(0, 140)
          };
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    const wData = hkoRes?.ok ? await hkoRes.json().catch(() => null) : null;
    if (wData && typeof wData === 'object') {
      for (const val of Object.values(wData)) {
        const code = String(val?.code || '').toUpperCase();
        if (!code) continue;
        weather.warningCodes.push(code);
        if (code === 'WRAINB' || code === 'WRAINR') {
          weather.heavyRain = true;
          weather.labelEn = code === 'WRAINB' ? 'Black Rainstorm' : 'Red Rainstorm';
          weather.labelTc = code === 'WRAINB' ? '黑色暴雨' : '紅色暴雨';
        } else if (code === 'WRAINA') {
          weather.rainAdvisory = true;
          if (!weather.labelEn) {
            weather.labelEn = 'Amber Rainstorm';
            weather.labelTc = '黃色暴雨';
          }
        } else if (/^TC(8|9|10)/.test(code)) {
          weather.heavyRain = true;
          weather.typhoon = true;
          weather.labelEn = `Typhoon ${code}`;
          weather.labelTc = '八號或以上風球';
        } else if (code === 'TC3') {
          weather.rainAdvisory = true;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  // Backward-compatible alias for older cached clients
  highwayCorridors.TM_CLK_TUNNEL = { ...highwayCorridors.LANTAU_TM, id: 'TM_CLK_TUNNEL' };

  return { highwayCorridors, weather };
}

export async function onRequest(context) {
  const { request } = context;
  if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: CORS_HEADERS });

  const url = new URL(request.url);
  const action = (url.searchParams.get('action') || '').toLowerCase();

  if (action === 'ferry_live') return handleFerryLiveRequest(url);

  if (action === 'lrt_live') {
    const stId = String(url.searchParams.get('station_id') || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
    const r = await fetchWithTimeout(`https://rt.data.gov.hk/v1/transport/mtr/lrt/getSchedule?station_id=${encodeURIComponent(stId)}`, 3500, {}, 10);
    const data = (r && r.ok) ? await r.json().catch(() => null) : null;
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(data || { status: 0, platform_list: [] }), { headers: CORS_HEADERS });
  }

  if (action === 'address_lookup' || action === 'als' || action === 'address') {
    return handleAddressLookup(url.searchParams.get('q') || '');
  }

  // Legacy endpoint slimmed to ferry CSV schedules only (full topology ships in bus_index.min.json)
  if (action === 'rail_ferry_topology') {
    const csvSchedules = await fetchOperatorFerryCsvSchedules();
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, slim: true, csvSchedules }), {
      headers: { ...CORS_HEADERS, 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=1800' }
    });
  }

  const { highwayCorridors, weather } = await syncTdStrategicTrafficCorridorsAndWeather();
  return new Response(
    JSON.stringify({ ok: true, service: 'transit-unified-edge', ts: Date.now(), highwayCorridors, weather }),
    { headers: { ...CORS_HEADERS, 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=60' } }
  );
}
```

const MTR_LINE_COLORS = {
  AEL: '#007078', TCL: '#F7943E', TML: '#9A3B26', TKL: '#7E3C93',
  EAL: '#53B7E8', SIL: '#B5BD00', TWL: '#E60012', ISL: '#0075C2',

};

const IOS_LRT_INDIGO = '#5856D6';
const HK_TRAM_ADULT_FARE = 3.0;

function getRouteColorHex(mode, company, routeNo) {
  if (mode === 'FERRY') return IOS_FERRY_BLUE;
  if (mode === 'MTR') {
    if (company === 'LRT') return IOS_LRT_INDIGO;
    return MTR_LINE_COLORS[String(routeNo || '').toUpperCase()] || IOS_MTR_BLUE;
  }
  return IOS_BUS_RED;
}

const INTER_ISLANDS_TEMP_NOTICE_EN = 'Temp Mui Wo N. Berth lift/ramp works schedule (from 26 Oct 2026, ~2.5 mos)';
const INTER_ISLANDS_TEMP_NOTICE_TC = '梅窩碼頭北面泊位升降台工程臨時時間表 (2026年10月26日起，約2個半月)';
const INTER_ISLANDS_REVIEW_AFTER   = '2027-01-10';

const _hm = (str) => {
  const [h, m] = str.split(':').map(Number);
  return h * 60 + m;
};
const _hmList = (csv, dur = 0) => csv.split(',').map(s => {
  const clean = s.trim();
  if (clean.includes('|')) {
    const [t, d] = clean.split('|');
    return [_hm(t), Number(d)];
  }
  return dur ? [_hm(clean), dur] : _hm(clean);
});
const _genCadence = (ranges, dur = 9) => {
  const out = [];
  for (const [sStr, eStr, step] of ranges) {
    for (let m = _hm(sStr); m <= _hm(eStr); m += step) out.push([m, dur]);
  }
  return out;
};

    sat: _hmList('07:35|28,08:57|28,10:36|18,12:36|18,14:51|18,16:36|18,17:36|18,18:42|28'),
    hol: _hmList('07:35|28,08:57|28,10:36|18,12:36|18,14:51|18,16:36|18,17:36|18,18:42|28')
  }
};
// ============================================================================
// SHARED NORMALIZATION, LRT RESOLVER & FERRY KEY HELPERS (Zero Drift)
// ============================================================================

function normName(s, stripPierSuffix = true) {
  let out = String(s || '')
    .replace(/<!\[CDATA\[([\s\S]*?)\]\]>/gi, '$1')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s*[\(（][^\)）]*[\)）]\s*/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+at\s+.*$/i, '')
    .split(',')[0]
    .replace(/\bcentral\s*(?:ferry\s*)?pier\s*(?:no\.?\s*)?\d+\b/gi, 'central')
    .replace(/\b(bus terminus|public transport interchange|pti|bbi|mtr station|light rail stop|light rail|mtr|railway station|train station|station|stop|halt)\b/gi, '')
    .replace(/(巴士總站|公共運輸交匯處|轉車站|港鐵站|輕鐵站|鐵路站|輕鐵|港鐵|車站|站)/g, '');
  if (stripPierSuffix) {
    out = out
      .replace(/\b(ferry pier|public pier|ferry terminal|promenade landing steps|landing steps|new development ferry pier|pier\s*no\.?\s*\d+|pier)\b/gi, '')
      .replace(/(新發展碼頭|海濱長廊登岸梯級|渡輪碼頭|公眾碼頭|客運碼頭|登岸梯級|碼頭|[一二三四五六七八九十\d]+號碼頭)/g, '');
  }
  return out.replace(/[^a-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fa5]/g, '');
}

const normKey = normName;

function _haversineKm(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {
  const n1 = Number(lat1), m1 = Number(lon1), n2 = Number(lat2), m2 = Number(lon2);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n1) || !Number.isFinite(m1) || !Number.isFinite(n2) || !Number.isFinite(m2)) return 999;
  const R = 6371;
  const dLat = (n2 - n1) * Math.PI / 180;
  const dLon = (m2 - m1) * Math.PI / 180;
  const a = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(n1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(n2 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;
  return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, a)), Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - a)));
}

const LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME = new Map();
for (const [id, info] of Object.entries(LRT_STATION_DIRECTORY)) {
  const cleanEn = info.en.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  const cleanTc = info.tc.replace(/[^\u4e00-\u9fa50-9]/g, '');
  if (cleanEn) LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(cleanEn, id);
  if (cleanTc) LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(cleanTc, id);
  if (info.code) LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(info.code.toLowerCase(), id);
  LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(normName(info.en, false), id);
  LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(normName(info.tc, false), id);
  if (info.altEn) {
    LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(info.altEn.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''), id);
    LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(normName(info.altEn, false), id);
  }
  if (info.altTc) {
    LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(info.altTc.replace(/[^\u4e00-\u9fa50-9]/g, ''), id);
    LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.set(normName(info.altTc, false), id);
  }
}

const MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME = new Map();
for (const [code, info] of Object.entries(MTR_STATION_DIRECTORY)) {
  MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.set(code.toLowerCase(), code);
  MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.set(normName(info.en, false), code);
  MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.set(normName(info.tc, false), code);
  MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.set(info.en.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''), code);
  MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.set(info.tc.replace(/[^\u4e00-\u9fa50-9]/g, ''), code);
}
[
  ['hongkonguniversity', 'HKU'], ['universityofhongkong', 'HKU'],
  ['asiaworldexpo', 'AWE'], ['lohaspark', 'LHP'],
  ['mongkok', 'MOK'], ['mongkokeast', 'MKK'],
  ['tsimshatsui', 'TST'], ['easttsimshatsui', 'ETS'],
  ['disneyland', 'DIS'], ['exhibition', 'EXC']
].forEach(([alias, code]) => { if (!MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.has(alias)) MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.set(alias, code); });

function resolveMtrStationCode(rawCodeOrName) {
  const s = String(rawCodeOrName || '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';
  const up = s.toUpperCase();
  if (MTR_STATION_DIRECTORY[up]) return up;
  const cleaned = normName(s, false);
  if (cleaned && MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.has(cleaned)) return MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.get(cleaned);
  const alnum = s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fa5]/g, '');
  if (alnum && MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.has(alnum)) return MTR_CODE_BY_NORM_NAME.get(alnum);
  return '';
}

function resolveLrtNumericId(code, nameEn = '', nameTc = '') {
  const numStr = String(parseInt(String(code || '').replace(/\D/g, ''), 10) || '');
  if (numStr && LRT_STATION_DIRECTORY[numStr]) return numStr;
  const cLow = String(code || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (cLow && LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.has(cLow)) return LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.get(cLow);
  const kEn = String(nameEn || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\b(light rail stop|light rail|stop|station)\b/gi, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  if (kEn && LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.has(kEn)) return LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.get(kEn);
  const kTc = String(nameTc || '').replace(/(輕鐵站|輕鐵|車站|站)/g, '').replace(/[^\u4e00-\u9fa50-9]/g, '');
  if (kTc && LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.has(kTc)) return LRT_ID_BY_NORM_NAME.get(kTc);
  return numStr || '';
}

function resolveFerryLiveEtaKey(origEn, destEn) {
  const o = normName(origEn, true), d = normName(destEn, true);
  if (o.includes('central') && d.includes('cheungchau')) return 'SF_CECC';
  if (o.includes('cheungchau') && d.includes('central')) return 'SF_CCCE';
  if (o.includes('central') && d.includes('muiwo')) return 'SF_CEMW';
  if (o.includes('muiwo') && d.includes('central')) return 'SF_MWCE';
  if (o.includes('northpoint') && d.includes('hunghom')) return 'SF_NPHH';
  if (o.includes('hunghom') && d.includes('northpoint')) return 'SF_HHNP';
  if (o.includes('northpoint') && d.includes('kowlooncity')) return 'SF_NPKC';
  if (o.includes('kowlooncity') && d.includes('northpoint')) return 'SF_KCNP';
  if (o.includes('pengchau') && d.includes('muiwo')) return 'SF_PCMW';
  if (o.includes('muiwo') && d.includes('pengchau')) return 'SF_MWPC';
  if (o.includes('cheungchau') && d.includes('muiwo')) return 'SF_CCMW';
  if (o.includes('muiwo') && d.includes('cheungchau')) return 'SF_MWCC';
  if (o.includes('central') && d.includes('sokkwuwan')) return 'HKKF_1_O';
  if (o.includes('sokkwuwan') && d.includes('central')) return 'HKKF_1_I';
  if (o.includes('central') && d.includes('yungshuewan')) return 'HKKF_2_O';
  if (o.includes('yungshuewan') && d.includes('central')) return 'HKKF_2_I';
  if (o.includes('central') && d.includes('pengchau')) return 'HKKF_3_O';
  if (o.includes('pengchau') && d.includes('central')) return 'HKKF_3_I';
  if (o.includes('pengchau') && d.includes('heilingchau')) return 'HKKF_4_O';
  if (o.includes('heilingchau') && d.includes('pengchau')) return 'HKKF_4_I';
  if (o.includes('central') && d.includes('hunghom')) return 'FF_CHH';
  if (o.includes('hunghom') && d.includes('central')) return 'FF_HHC';
  if (o.includes('northpoint') && d.includes('kwuntong')) return 'FF_NPKT';
  if (o.includes('kwuntong') && d.includes('northpoint')) return 'FF_KTNP';
  if (o.includes('tuenmun') && (d.includes('taio') || d.includes('tungchung') || d.includes('shalowan'))) return 'FF_TMTO';
  if ((o.includes('taio') || o.includes('tungchung') || o.includes('shalowan')) && d.includes('tuenmun')) return 'FF_TOTM';
  return null;
}

function mergeOperatingWindows(rawWindows) {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawWindows) || !rawWindows.length) return [];
  const valid = rawWindows
    .filter(w => Array.isArray(w) && Number.isFinite(w[0]) && Number.isFinite(w[1]))
    .map(([s, e]) => [Math.max(0, Math.min(1439, Math.round(s))), Math.max(0, Math.min(1439, Math.round(e)))])
    .filter(([s, e]) => e >= s)
    .sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
  if (!valid.length) return [];
  const merged = [valid[0]];
  for (let i = 1; i < valid.length; i++) {
    const prev = merged[merged.length - 1];
    const cur = valid[i];
    if (cur[0] <= prev[1] + 45) {
      prev[1] = Math.max(prev[1], cur[1]);
    } else {
      merged.push(cur);
    }
  }
  return merged;
}

function deriveFerryActiveWindowsFromSched(sched) {
  if (!sched) return [[330, 1439], [0, 30]];
  const allMins = [];
  ['wk', 'sat', 'hol'].forEach(k => {
    (sched[k] || []).forEach(item => {
      const m = Array.isArray(item) ? Number(item[0]) : Number(item);
      if (Number.isFinite(m) && m >= 0 && m < 1440) allMins.push(m);
    });
  });
  if (!allMins.length) return [[330, 1439], [0, 30]];
  const sorted = [...new Set(allMins)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const rawWins = [];
  let start = sorted[0], prev = sorted[0];
  for (let i = 1; i < sorted.length; i++) {
    if (sorted[i] - prev <= 150) {
      prev = sorted[i];
    } else {
      rawWins.push([Math.max(0, start - 15), Math.min(1439, prev + 35)]);
      start = sorted[i];
      prev = sorted[i];
    }
  }
  rawWins.push([Math.max(0, start - 15), Math.min(1439, prev + 35)]);
  return mergeOperatingWindows(rawWins);
}

  isClosedAt(dateOrTimeStr, isOvernightHoliday = false) {
    if (isOvernightHoliday) return false;
    let mins = -1;
    if (dateOrTimeStr instanceof Date) {
      mins = dateOrTimeStr.getHours() * 60 + dateOrTimeStr.getMinutes();
    } else if (typeof dateOrTimeStr === 'string' && dateOrTimeStr.includes(':')) {
      const [h, m] = dateOrTimeStr.split(':').map(Number);
      if (Number.isFinite(h) && Number.isFinite(m)) mins = (h % 24) * 60 + m;
    }
    if (mins < 0) return false;
    return mins >= this.RULES.operatingHours.closedStartMin && mins <= this.RULES.operatingHours.closedEndMin;
  },

  getStationDepthSec(staCode) {
    const c = resolveMtrStationCode(staCode) || String(staCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return this.STATION_DEPTH[c] || 90;
  },

  getInterchangeWalkSec(staCode, lineA, lineB, nextStaCode = '') {
    const c1 = resolveMtrStationCode(staCode) || String(staCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const c2 = resolveMtrStationCode(nextStaCode) || String(nextStaCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if ((c1 === 'TST' && c2 === 'ETS') || (c1 === 'ETS' && c2 === 'TST')) {
      return this.INTERCHANGE_TIME.TST_ETS_OSI;
    }
    if ((c1 === 'CEN' && c2 === 'HOK') || (c1 === 'HOK' && c2 === 'CEN')) {
      return this.INTERCHANGE_TIME.CEN_HOK_WALK;
    }
    const lA = String(lineA || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const lB = String(lineB || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (lA === 'LRT' || lB === 'LRT') return this.INTERCHANGE_TIME.LRT_TML_XFER;
    const pair = [lA, lB].sort().join('_');
    const exact = this.INTERCHANGE_TIME[`${c1}_${pair}`];
    if (exact) return exact;
    if (c1 === 'MEF' || c1 === 'QUB' || c1 === 'CEN' || c1 === 'HOK') return 300;
    if (c1 === 'KOT' || c1 === 'DIH' || c1 === 'HUH' || c1 === 'TAW') return 150;
    return 120;
  },

  getAdjacentRunningTimeSec(fromCode, toCode) {
    const a = resolveMtrStationCode(fromCode) || String(fromCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const b = resolveMtrStationCode(toCode) || String(toCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!a || !b || a === b) return 0;
    const mapped = MTR_ADJACENT_RUNNING_TIME[`${a}_${b}`];
    if (mapped) return mapped;
    const stA = MTR_STATION_DIRECTORY[a], stB = MTR_STATION_DIRECTORY[b];
    if (stA && stB) {
      const dKm = _haversineKm(stA.lat, stA.lon, stB.lat, stB.lon);
      if (Number.isFinite(dKm) && dKm < 30) {
        const speedMps = dKm >= 2.5 ? 21.5 : (dKm >= 1.4 ? 17.5 : 14.0);
        return Math.max(75, Math.round((dKm * 1000 * 1.05) / speedMps) + 25);
      }
    }
    return 150;
  },

  isTerminusStation(staCode) {
    const c = resolveMtrStationCode(staCode) || String(staCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return this.TERMINUS_STATIONS.has(c);
  },

  isFreeMtrFeederBus(routeNo, mtrStationCode = '', isHoliday = false) {
    const r = String(routeNo || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const rule = this.MTR_FEEDER_BUS_STATIONS[r];
    if (!rule) return false;
    if (isHoliday && rule.noHolidayFree) return false;
    if (!mtrStationCode) return true;
    const st = resolveMtrStationCode(mtrStationCode) || String(mtrStationCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!st) return true;
    return rule.stations.includes(st);
  },

  checkFareSaverAtOrigin(lat, lon, entryStaCode, destStaCode = '') {
    const la = Number(lat), lo = Number(lon);
    const entry = resolveMtrStationCode(entryStaCode) || String(entryStaCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const dest = resolveMtrStationCode(destStaCode) || String(destStaCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!Number.isFinite(la) || !Number.isFinite(lo) || !entry) return null;
    if (this.RULES.exclusions.crossBoundary.has(dest) || this.RULES.exclusions.airportExpress.has(dest) || this.RULES.exclusions.airportExpress.has(entry)) {
      return null;
    }
    for (const fs of this.FARE_SAVERS) {
      if (!fs.targetStations.includes(entry)) continue;
      if (_haversineKm(la, lo, fs.lat, fs.lon) <= 0.14) return fs;
    }
    return null;
  },

  lookupExactHeavyRailFare(origCode, destCode, matrixLookupFn = null) {
    const a = resolveMtrStationCode(origCode) || String(origCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const b = resolveMtrStationCode(destCode) || String(destCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!a || !b) return 0;
    if (a === b) return 5.0;

    const aelFare = this.AEL_FARES[`${a}_${b}`];
    if (aelFare > 0) return aelFare;

    if (typeof matrixLookupFn === 'function') {
      const exact = Number(matrixLookupFn(a, b) || 0);
      if (exact > 0) return Number(exact.toFixed(1));
    } else if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.getExactMtrFare === 'function') {
      const exact = Number(window.getExactMtrFare(a, b) || 0);
      if (exact > 0) return Number(exact.toFixed(1));
    }

    const stA = MTR_STATION_DIRECTORY[a], stB = MTR_STATION_DIRECTORY[b];
    if (!stA || !stB) return 0;
    const dKm = _haversineKm(stA.lat, stA.lon, stB.lat, stB.lon);
    const isCrossBoundary = this.RULES.exclusions.crossBoundary.has(a) || this.RULES.exclusions.crossBoundary.has(b);
    const isIslandA = stA.lat < 22.293 && stA.lon >= 114.115 && stA.lon <= 114.265;
    const isIslandB = stB.lat < 22.293 && stB.lon >= 114.115 && stB.lon <= 114.265;
    const crossesHarbour = (isIslandA !== isIslandB);

    if (isCrossBoundary) {
      return Number(Math.min(65.4, Math.max(26.5, 25.0 + dKm * 1.15 + (crossesHarbour ? 6.5 : 0))).toFixed(1));
    }
    let est = 4.9 + dKm * 0.92 + (crossesHarbour ? 4.8 : 0);
    if (a === 'TUC' || b === 'TUC') est = Math.max(est, 16.5 + (crossesHarbour ? 6.5 : 0));
    if (a === 'DIS' || b === 'DIS') est = Math.max(est, 14.5 + (crossesHarbour ? 6.5 : 0));
    return Number(Math.min(34.0, Math.max(4.9, est)).toFixed(1));
  },

  calculateFirstClassSurcharge(ealFromCode, ealToCode, matrixLookupFn = null) {
    const a = resolveMtrStationCode(ealFromCode) || String(ealFromCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const b = resolveMtrStationCode(ealToCode) || String(ealToCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!a || !b || a === b) return 0;
    const ealStd = this.lookupExactHeavyRailFare(a, b, matrixLookupFn);
    return Number(Math.max(4.9, ealStd).toFixed(1));
  },

  evaluateRailChain(railLegs, options = {}) {
    if (!Array.isArray(railLegs) || !railLegs.length) {
      return { status: 'EMPTY', totalFare: 0, totalTimeSec: 0 };
    }
    if (options.searchDate && this.isClosedAt(options.searchDate, Boolean(options.isOvernightHoliday))) {
      return { status: 'CLOSED', error: 'MTR is not operating at this time.', totalFare: 0, totalTimeSec: 0 };
    }

    const matrixFn = options.matrixLookupFn || null;
    const isHeavy = (l) => l && l.company !== 'LRT' && l.line !== 'LRT';
    const heavyLegs = railLegs.filter(isHeavy);
    const lrtLegs = railLegs.filter(l => !isHeavy(l));

    let totalTimeSec = 0;
    const firstLeg = railLegs[0];
    const lastLeg = railLegs[railLegs.length - 1];
    if (isHeavy(firstLeg)) totalTimeSec += this.getStationDepthSec(firstLeg.from);

    for (let i = 0; i < railLegs.length; i++) {
      const leg = railLegs[i];
      if (Number.isFinite(leg.driveSec) && leg.driveSec > 0) {
        totalTimeSec += leg.driveSec;
      } else if (Array.isArray(leg.stopsSeq) && leg.stopsSeq.length >= 2) {
        for (let k = 0; k < leg.stopsSeq.length - 1; k++) {
          totalTimeSec += this.getAdjacentRunningTimeSec(leg.stopsSeq[k], leg.stopsSeq[k + 1]);
        }
      } else {
        totalTimeSec += this.getAdjacentRunningTimeSec(leg.from, leg.to);
      }

      const hwSec = (leg.line === 'AEL' || leg.line === 'DRL') ? 600 : (leg.company === 'LRT' ? 420 : 210);
      totalTimeSec += Math.round(hwSec / 2);

      if (i < railLegs.length - 1) {
        const nextLeg = railLegs[i + 1];
        const xferSec = Number.isFinite(leg.walkAfterSec) && leg.walkAfterSec > 0
          ? leg.walkAfterSec
          : this.getInterchangeWalkSec(leg.to, leg.line, nextLeg.line, nextLeg.from);
        totalTimeSec += xferSec;
      }
    }
    if (isHeavy(lastLeg)) totalTimeSec += this.getStationDepthSec(lastLeg.to);

    let totalFare = 0;
    let fareSaverApplied = null;
    let osiSplitApplied = false;

    if (heavyLegs.length > 0) {
      const absOrigin = resolveMtrStationCode(heavyLegs[0].from) || String(heavyLegs[0].from || '').toUpperCase();
      const absDest = resolveMtrStationCode(heavyLegs[heavyLegs.length - 1].to) || String(heavyLegs[heavyLegs.length - 1].to || '').toUpperCase();

      let splitIdx = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < heavyLegs.length - 1; i++) {
        const a = resolveMtrStationCode(heavyLegs[i].to) || String(heavyLegs[i].to || '').toUpperCase();
        const b = resolveMtrStationCode(heavyLegs[i + 1].from) || String(heavyLegs[i + 1].from || '').toUpperCase();
        if ((a === 'TST' && b === 'ETS') || (a === 'ETS' && b === 'TST')) {
          const walkMins = ((heavyLegs[i].walkAfterSec || this.INTERCHANGE_TIME.TST_ETS_OSI) / 60);
          if (walkMins > this.RULES.osi.tstEtsLimitMins) {
            splitIdx = i;
            osiSplitApplied = true;
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      if (splitIdx >= 0) {
        const f1 = this.lookupExactHeavyRailFare(absOrigin, heavyLegs[splitIdx].to, matrixFn);
        const f2 = this.lookupExactHeavyRailFare(heavyLegs[splitIdx + 1].from, absDest, matrixFn);
        totalFare = f1 + f2;
      } else if (absOrigin === absDest) {
        totalFare = totalTimeSec <= this.RULES.bylaws.sameStationShortSecs
          ? 5.0
          : this.RULES.bylaws.sameStationLongPenaltyHkd;
      } else {
        totalFare = this.lookupExactHeavyRailFare(absOrigin, absDest, matrixFn);
      }

      if (totalTimeSec > this.RULES.bylaws.maxPaidAreaSecs && totalFare < this.RULES.bylaws.maxOverstayFareHkd) {
        totalFare = this.RULES.bylaws.maxOverstayFareHkd;
      }

      if (options.isFirstClass) {
        const ealLeg = heavyLegs.find(l => String(l.line || '').toUpperCase() === 'EAL');
        if (ealLeg) {
          totalFare += this.calculateFirstClassSurcharge(ealLeg.from, ealLeg.to, matrixFn);
        }
      }

      if (Number.isFinite(options.originLat) && Number.isFinite(options.originLon)) {
        const fs = this.checkFareSaverAtOrigin(options.originLat, options.originLon, absOrigin, absDest);
        if (fs) {
          fareSaverApplied = fs;
          totalFare = Math.max(0, totalFare - fs.discount);
        }
      }
    }

    if (lrtLegs.length > 0) {
      let lrtRawFare = 0;
      for (const ll of lrtLegs) {
        lrtRawFare += Number(ll.lrtFareHkd || 5.2);
      }
      if (heavyLegs.length > 0) {
        const touchesNwntHub = heavyLegs.some(hl =>
          this.RULES.lrtInterchange.stations.has(resolveMtrStationCode(hl.from) || hl.from) ||
          this.RULES.lrtInterchange.stations.has(resolveMtrStationCode(hl.to) || hl.to)
        );
        if (touchesNwntHub) {
          const deduction = Math.min(lrtRawFare, this.RULES.lrtInterchange.maxDeductionHkd);
          totalFare += Math.max(0, lrtRawFare - deduction);
        } else {
          totalFare += lrtRawFare;
        }
      } else {
        totalFare += lrtRawFare;
      }
    }

    return {
      status: 'OK',
      totalFare: Number(totalFare.toFixed(1)),
      totalTimeSec: Math.round(totalTimeSec),
      totalTimeMins: Math.ceil(totalTimeSec / 60),
      fareSaverApplied: fareSaverApplied ? fareSaverApplied.id : null,
      osiSplitApplied
    };
  }
};

// ============================================================================
// MTR FLEET CONFIGURATION & BUS/MTR PASSENGER LOAD FACTOR INFERENCE ENGINE
// (Explicitly excludes FERRY — users cannot switch cars or wait 2m for next boat)
// ============================================================================

const MTR_FLEET_CONFIG = {
  EAL: { totalCars: 9, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 1, stdCapPax: 2600, hasFirstClass: true,  peakBaseHwSec: 165, offPeakHwSec: 270 },
  TWL: { totalCars: 8, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 2500, hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 120, offPeakHwSec: 240 },
  ISL: { totalCars: 8, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 2500, hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 120, offPeakHwSec: 240 },
  KTL: { totalCars: 8, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 2500, hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 130, offPeakHwSec: 240 },
  TKL: { totalCars: 8, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 2500, hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 150, offPeakHwSec: 270 },
  TCL: { totalCars: 8, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 2600, hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 210, offPeakHwSec: 360 },
  TML: { totalCars: 8, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 2700, hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 165, offPeakHwSec: 300 },
  SIL: { totalCars: 3, standardCars: 3, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 900,  hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 200, offPeakHwSec: 300 },
  DRL: { totalCars: 4, standardCars: 4, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 1000, hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 600, offPeakHwSec: 600 },
  AEL: { totalCars: 8, standardCars: 8, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 480,  hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 600, offPeakHwSec: 600, allSeated: true },
  LRT: { totalCars: 1, standardCars: 1, firstClassCars: 0, stdCapPax: 210,  hasFirstClass: false, peakBaseHwSec: 300, offPeakHwSec: 450 }
};

const MTR_BOTTLENECK_STATIONS = {
  TAW: { boostPeak: 26, boostOff: 10, lines: ['EAL', 'TML'] },
  KOT: { boostPeak: 24, boostOff: 10, lines: ['EAL', 'KTL'] },
  ADM: { boostPeak: 24, boostOff: 10, lines: ['TWL', 'ISL', 'EAL', 'SIL'] },
  MOK: { boostPeak: 20, boostOff: 9,  lines: ['TWL', 'KTL'] },
  PRE: { boostPeak: 20, boostOff: 9,  lines: ['TWL', 'KTL'] },
  QUB: { boostPeak: 18, boostOff: 7,  lines: ['ISL', 'TKL'] },
  NOP: { boostPeak: 16, boostOff: 6,  lines: ['ISL', 'TKL'] },
  NAC: { boostPeak: 18, boostOff: 7,  lines: ['TCL', 'TML'] },
  MEF: { boostPeak: 16, boostOff: 6,  lines: ['TWL', 'TML'] },
  HUH: { boostPeak: 18, boostOff: 7,  lines: ['EAL', 'TML'] },
  CEN: { boostPeak: 16, boostOff: 6,  lines: ['ISL', 'TWL'] },
  TKO: { boostPeak: 16, boostOff: 6,  lines: ['TKL'] },
  YAT: { boostPeak: 16, boostOff: 6,  lines: ['KTL', 'TKL'] },
  TST: { boostPeak: 15, boostOff: 8,  lines: ['TWL'] },
  CAB: { boostPeak: 15, boostOff: 8,  lines: ['ISL'] },
  SHT: { boostPeak: 14, boostOff: 6,  lines: ['EAL'] }
};

const LoadFactorEngine = {
  _toBand(pct, mode = 'BUS') {
    const p = Math.max(8, Math.min(99, Math.round(pct)));
    if (p < 48) {
      return {
        pct: p,
        tier: 'LOW',
        cssClass: 'load-low',
        labelEn: 'Seats',
        labelTc: mode === 'MTR' ? '寬鬆' : '有位',
        isSkipRisk: false,
        isStandingOrWorse: false
      };
    }
    if (p < 75) {
      return {
        pct: p,
        tier: 'MED',
        cssClass: 'load-med',
        labelEn: 'Standing',
        labelTc: mode === 'MTR' ? '一般' : '企位',
        isSkipRisk: false,
        isStandingOrWorse: true
      };
    }
    if (p < 90) {
      return {
        pct: p,
        tier: 'HIGH',
        cssClass: 'load-high',
        labelEn: 'Packed',
        labelTc: '擠迫',
        isSkipRisk: false,
        isStandingOrWorse: true
      };
    }
    return {
      pct: p,
      tier: 'FULL',
      cssClass: 'load-full',
      labelEn: mode === 'MTR' ? 'Very Crowded' : 'Near Full',
      labelTc: mode === 'MTR' ? '非常擠迫' : '近乎客滿',
      isSkipRisk: true,
      isStandingOrWorse: true
    };
  },

  _getPeriodProfile(dateObj, isHoliday = false) {
    const d = dateObj instanceof Date ? dateObj : new Date();
    const mins = d.getHours() * 60 + d.getMinutes();
    const day = d.getDay();
    const isWkndOrHol = Boolean(isHoliday) || day === 0 || day === 6;

    if (!isWkndOrHol && ((mins >= 450 && mins <= 565) || (mins >= 1045 && mins <= 1175))) {
      return { period: 'PEAK', baseBusPct: 54, baseMtrPct: 62, isPeak: true };
    }
    if ((mins >= 720 && mins <= 840) || (isWkndOrHol && mins >= 660 && mins <= 1140)) {
      return { period: 'BUSY', baseBusPct: 40, baseMtrPct: 46, isPeak: false };
    }
    if (mins >= 1350 || mins < 390) {
      return { period: 'LATE', baseBusPct: 18, baseMtrPct: 20, isPeak: false };
    }
    return { period: 'OFF_PEAK', baseBusPct: 30, baseMtrPct: 34, isPeak: false };
  },

  inferBusLoad(opts = {}) {
    const g = opts.group;
    if (!g || g.mode === 'FERRY' || g.mode === 'MTR') return null;

    const co = String(g.company || '').toUpperCase();
    const rNo = String(g.routeNo || '').toUpperCase();
    const isGmb = co.startsWith('GMB');
    const isSingleDeck = isGmb || (co === 'NLB' && /^(1|2|3M|4|11|21|23|34|36)$/.test(rNo));
    const capacityFactor = isGmb ? 1.38 : (isSingleDeck ? 1.15 : 1.0);

    const prof = this._getPeriodProfile(opts.dateObj, opts.isHoliday);
    let score = prof.baseBusPct * capacityFactor;

    // 1. Stop-sequence progression curve (empty near terminus, peaking mid-corridor / major BBI hubs)
    const stopIdx = Number.isFinite(opts.stopIdx) ? opts.stopIdx : 0;
    const totalStops = Math.max(2, Number(opts.totalStops) || 12);
    const ratio = stopIdx / Math.max(1, totalStops - 1);
    if (stopIdx <= 1) {
      score -= 18;
    } else if (ratio >= 0.25 && ratio <= 0.72) {
      score += prof.isPeak ? 12 : 6;
    } else if (ratio > 0.85) {
      score -= 12;
    }

    if (opts.stopObj && typeof getMajorBbiHubMatch === 'function' && getMajorBbiHubMatch(opts.stopObj)) {
      score += prof.isPeak ? 12 : 6;
    }

    // Cross-harbour & trunk highway routes pack faster during peak
    if (/^(1\d{2}|6\d{2}|9\d{2}|E\d{2}|268|269|277|278|960|968|978)/.test(rNo) && prof.isPeak) {
      score += 8;
    }

    // 2. Headway Expansion Ratio (H_obs / H_sched): wider gap => leading bus absorbs accumulated demand
    const obsHw = Number(opts.obsHeadwaySec || 0);
    const schedHw = Math.max(180, Number(opts.schedHeadwaySec || ((Number(g.headwayMins) || 12) * 60)));
    if (obsHw > 60 && schedHw > 0) {
      const hwRatio = obsHw / schedHw;
      if (hwRatio >= 1.9) score += 24;
      else if (hwRatio >= 1.45) score += 14;
      else if (hwRatio <= 0.65) score -= 12; // Trailing bunched bus is noticeably emptier
    }

    // 3. Live ETA-Drift Residual Dwell normalized by corridor road speed
    const driftSec = Number(opts.etaDriftSec || 0);
    const corStatus = String(opts.corridorStatus || 'SMOOTH').toUpperCase();
    if (driftSec >= 40) {
      if (corStatus === 'SMOOTH') {
        // High road speed + ETA slipping backwards = boarding/alighting dwell bottleneck
        score += Math.min(22, Math.round((driftSec - 30) * 0.28));
      } else {
        // Corridor incident/slow traffic: discount drift so congestion isn't misread as full bus
        score += Math.min(7, Math.round((driftSec - 30) * 0.08));
      }
    }

    // 4. Live HKO Weather Demand Surge (rain spikes bus load 15–22%)
    const wx = opts.weather || (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.__HKO_WEATHER__ : null);
    if (wx?.heavyRain) score += 20;
    else if (wx?.rainAdvisory) score += 12;

    const band = this._toBand(score, 'BUS');
    let tipEn = '', tipTc = '';
    if (isGmb && band.pct >= 75) {
      tipEn = '19-seat GMB — high chance of full/skip';
      tipTc = '19座小巴 — 較大機會客滿飛站';
    } else if (band.isSkipRisk) {
      tipEn = 'High load inferred — check next departure';
      tipTc = '推算載客率偏高 — 可留意下一班車';
    }

    return {
      ...band,
      mode: 'BUS',
      capacityPax: isGmb ? 19 : (isSingleDeck ? 65 : 130),
      tipEn,
      tipTc,
      firstClassWarning: false
    };
  },

  inferMtrLoad(opts = {}) {
    const isLrt = Boolean(opts.isLrt || opts.company === 'LRT');
    const lineCode = isLrt ? 'LRT' : String(opts.lineCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const fleet = MTR_FLEET_CONFIG[lineCode] || MTR_FLEET_CONFIG.TWL;
    const staCode = resolveMtrStationCode(opts.staCode) || String(opts.staCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const prof = this._getPeriodProfile(opts.dateObj, opts.isHoliday);

    let score = prof.baseMtrPct;
    if (fleet.allSeated) score = Math.min(score, 32); // AEL dedicated airport express

    // 1. EAL 9-Car First Class Capacity Isolation (8 standard cars instead of 9 / legacy 12)
    if (lineCode === 'EAL') {
      score += prof.isPeak ? 12 : 5;
    }

    // 2. LRT 1-Car vs 2-Car live formation denominator
    const lrtCars = Number(opts.lrtTrainLength || 0);
    if (isLrt) {
      if (lrtCars === 1) score += prof.isPeak ? 18 : 8;
      else if (lrtCars >= 2) score -= 12;
    }

    // 3. Terminus vs. Bottleneck Pinch-Point Mapping
    if (!isLrt && staCode) {
      if (MTR_ENGINE.isTerminusStation(staCode)) {
        score -= 22;
      } else {
        const bp = MTR_BOTTLENECK_STATIONS[staCode];
        if (bp && (!bp.lines || bp.lines.includes(lineCode))) {
          score += prof.isPeak ? bp.boostPeak : bp.boostOff;
        }
      }
    }

    // 4. Live Next Train API Headway Expansion (Platform Accumulation Proxy)
    const obsHw = Number(opts.obsHeadwaySec || 0);
    const baseHw = prof.isPeak ? fleet.peakBaseHwSec : fleet.offPeakHwSec;
    if (obsHw > 60 && baseHw > 0) {
      const hwRatio = obsHw / baseHw;
      if (hwRatio >= 1.85) score += 26;
      else if (hwRatio >= 1.40) score += 14;
      else if (hwRatio <= 0.75) score -= 8;
    }

    // 5. EAL Arrival-to-Departure Dwell Delta (timetype "A" -> "D")
    const ealDwellSec = Number(opts.ealDwellDeltaSec || 0);
    if (lineCode === 'EAL' && ealDwellSec >= 45) {
      score = Math.max(score + 14, 86);
    }

    // 6. Live MTR isdelay === 'Y' flag -> immediate maximum downstream accumulation
    const isDelayed = String(opts.isDelay || '').toUpperCase() === 'Y' || opts.isDelay === true;
    if (isDelayed) {
      score = Math.max(score + 24, 92);
    }

    // 7. Rain surge into underground stations
    const wx = opts.weather || (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.__HKO_WEATHER__ : null);
    if (wx?.heavyRain) score += 12;
    else if (wx?.rainAdvisory) score += 6;

    const band = this._toBand(score, 'MTR');

    // Determine EAL direction for exact First Class Car # (Car 4 Southbound to ADM / Car 6 Northbound to LOW/LMC)
    let firstClassWarning = false;
    let firstClassCarNo = 0;
    let tipEn = '', tipTc = '';

    if (lineCode === 'EAL') {
      firstClassWarning = true;
      const destC = String(opts.destCode || '').toUpperCase();
      const destStr = String(opts.destName || '').toLowerCase();
      const isSouthbound = (destC === 'ADM' || destC === 'EXC' || destC === 'HUH' || /admiralty|hung hom|金鐘|紅磡/.test(destStr))
        || (!destC && !destStr && opts.dirCode === 'I');
      firstClassCarNo = isSouthbound ? 4 : 6;

      if (band.pct >= 75) {
        tipEn = `9-car train (8 std cars) · Car ${firstClassCarNo} is 1st Class ($1,000 surcharge) — Cars 3/5/7 bottleneck, try Cars 1–2 or 8–9`;
        tipTc = `9卡車（僅8卡普通等）· 第${firstClassCarNo}卡為頭等（未拍卡罰$1,000）— 第3/5/7卡最迫，建議上第1–2或8–9卡`;
      } else {
        tipEn = `9-car train · Car ${firstClassCarNo} is 1st Class (tap platform processor or $1,000 surcharge)`;
        tipTc = `9卡車 · 第${firstClassCarNo}卡為頭等車廂（上車前須於月台核准機拍卡，否則罰$1,000）`;
      }
    } else if (!isLrt && fleet.totalCars >= 8 && !fleet.allSeated && band.pct >= 75) {
      tipEn = `8-car train · Middle cars crowded — try Car 1 or Car ${fleet.totalCars} (~10–15% lighter)`;
      tipTc = `8卡車 · 中間車廂較擠迫 — 建議移步第1或第${fleet.totalCars}卡（較鬆動約10–15%）`;
    } else if (isLrt && lrtCars === 1 && band.pct >= 75) {
      tipEn = 'Single-car LRT — expect tighter standing room';
      tipTc = '單卡輕鐵 — 較為擠迫';
    }

    return {
      ...band,
      mode: 'MTR',
      lineCode,
      totalCars: isLrt ? (lrtCars || 1) : fleet.totalCars,
      standardCars: isLrt ? (lrtCars || 1) : fleet.standardCars,
      firstClassWarning,
      firstClassCarNo,
      isDelayed,
      tipEn,
      tipTc
    };
  }
};

function getMajorBbiHubMatch(stopObj) {
  if (!stopObj) return null;
  const txt = `${stopObj.stopName_e || ''} ${stopObj.name_tc || ''}`;
  const la = parseFloat(stopObj.lat ?? stopObj.latitude);
  const lo = parseFloat(stopObj.lon ?? stopObj.longitude);
  for (const hub of MAJOR_BBI_HUBS) {
    if (hub.re.test(txt)) return hub;
    if (Number.isFinite(la) && Number.isFinite(lo) && _haversineKm(la, lo, hub.lat, hub.lon) <= hub.radiusKm) {
      return hub;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function resolveRelevantCorridorForTrip(fromLat, fromLon, toLat, toLon) {
  const fLa = Number(fromLat), fLo = Number(fromLon), tLa = Number(toLat), tLo = Number(toLon);
  if (!Number.isFinite(fLa) || !Number.isFinite(tLa)) return null;
  const dKm = _haversineKm(fLa, fLo, tLa, tLo);
  if (dKm < 2.5) return null;

  const isHkA = fLa < 22.293 && fLo >= 114.110 && fLo <= 114.265;
  const isHkB = tLa < 22.293 && tLo >= 114.110 && tLo <= 114.265;
  if (isHkA !== isHkB) return HK_STRATEGIC_CORRIDORS.find(c => c.id === 'HARBOUR_TUNS');

  const isLanA = fLo < 114.055 && fLa < 22.345;
  const isLanB = tLo < 114.055 && tLa < 22.345;
  if (isLanA !== isLanB) return HK_STRATEGIC_CORRIDORS.find(c => c.id === 'LANTAU_TM');

  const samplePts = [
    [(fLa + tLa) / 2, (fLo + tLo) / 2],
    [fLa * 0.7 + tLa * 0.3, fLo * 0.7 + tLo * 0.3],
    [fLa * 0.3 + tLa * 0.7, fLo * 0.3 + tLo * 0.7]
  ];
  let bestCor = null, bestDist = Infinity;
  for (const cor of HK_STRATEGIC_CORRIDORS) {
    for (const [sLa, sLo] of samplePts) {
      const d = _haversineKm(sLa, sLo, cor.lat, cor.lon);
      if (d <= cor.radiusKm && d < bestDist) {
        bestDist = d;
        bestCor = cor;
      }
    }
  }
  return bestCor;
}

function getSectionalFareMeta(company, routeNo) {
  const co = String(company || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const rNo = String(routeNo || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  return SECTIONAL_FARE_REGISTRY[`${co}_${rNo}`] || null;
}

function resolveBusLegNetFare(group, boardStop, alightStop = null, isHoliday = false) {
  if (!group || !boardStop) {
    return { upfrontFare: 0, netFare: 0, rebateAmount: 0, rebateType: null, noticeEn: '', noticeTc: '' };
  }
  const co = String(group.company || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const rNo = String(group.routeNo || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const meta = getSectionalFareMeta(co, rNo);

  const rawUpfrontStr = (isHoliday && boardStop.fareHol && parseFloat(boardStop.fareHol) > 0)
    ? boardStop.fareHol
    : boardStop.fare;
  let upfrontFare = parseFloat(rawUpfrontStr || '0');
  if (!Number.isFinite(upfrontFare) || upfrontFare <= 0) {
    upfrontFare = Number(group.maxFare || 0);
  }
  let netFare = upfrontFare;
  let rebateAmount = 0;
  let noticeEn = '';
  let noticeTc = '';

  if (!meta) {
    return { upfrontFare, netFare, rebateAmount: 0, rebateType: null, noticeEn: '', noticeTc: '' };
  }

  const rId = String(group.routeIds?.[0] || group.key || '');
  const destStr = `${group.dest_e || ''} ${group.dest_tc || ''}`;
  let dir = (rId.includes('_I') || String(group.key || '').endsWith('_I')) ? 'I' : 'O';

  if (co === 'NLB' && rNo === '3M') {
    if (/tung\s*chung|東涌/i.test(destStr)) dir = 'I';
    else if (/mui\s*wo|梅窩/i.test(destStr)) dir = 'O';
  }

  const bSeq = Number(boardStop.seq || 0);
  const aSeq = Number(alightStop?.seq || 0);
  const boardName  = `${boardStop.stopName_e || ''} ${boardStop.name_tc || ''}`;
  const alightName = `${alightStop?.stopName_e || ''} ${alightStop?.name_tc || ''}`;
  const bLat = parseFloat(boardStop.lat ?? boardStop.latitude);
  const bLon = parseFloat(boardStop.lon ?? boardStop.longitude);
  const aLat = parseFloat(alightStop?.lat ?? alightStop?.latitude);
  const aLon = parseFloat(alightStop?.lon ?? alightStop?.longitude);
  const hopKm = (Number.isFinite(bLat) && Number.isFinite(aLat)) ? _haversineKm(bLat, bLon, aLat, aLon) : 99;

  if (meta.rebateType === 'strict_boarding_only') {
    const isTungChungBoard = /tung\s*chung|東涌/i.test(boardName) && (bSeq === 0 || bSeq <= 4);
    const isToTaiO = /tai\s*o|大澳/i.test(destStr);
    if (isTungChungBoard && isToTaiO) {
      const locked = isHoliday ? meta.lockedOutboundFareHol : meta.lockedOutboundFareWk;
      if (locked > 0) {
        upfrontFare = Math.max(upfrontFare, locked);
        netFare = upfrontFare;
      }
      if (alightStop && hopKm < 7.5) {
        noticeEn = meta.noticeEn;
        noticeTc = meta.noticeTc;
      }
    }
    return { upfrontFare: Number(upfrontFare.toFixed(1)), netFare: Number(netFare.toFixed(1)), rebateAmount: 0, rebateType: meta.rebateType, noticeEn, noticeTc };
  }

  if (meta.twoWayTiers && alightStop) {
    const tiers = meta.twoWayTiers[dir] || [];
    for (const tier of tiers) {
      if (tier.excludeBoardRe && tier.excludeBoardRe.test(boardName)) continue;
      if (tier.excludeAlightRe && tier.excludeAlightRe.test(alightName)) continue;
      if (tier.maxBoardSeq && bSeq > 0 && bSeq > tier.maxBoardSeq) continue;

      const seqHit = (aSeq > 0 && bSeq > 0 && bSeq < aSeq && aSeq <= tier.maxAlightSeq);
      const nameHit = (aSeq === 0 && tier.alightRe && tier.alightRe.test(alightName));
      if (seqHit || nameHit) {
        const candNet = isHoliday ? tier.hol : tier.wk;
        if (candNet > 0 && candNet < upfrontFare - 0.1) {
          netFare = candNet;
          rebateAmount = Number((upfrontFare - netFare).toFixed(1));
          noticeEn = meta.noticeEn;
          noticeTc = meta.noticeTc;
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  } else if (meta.rebateType === 'driver_override' && alightStop && hopKm < 6.5 && upfrontFare > 6.5) {
    const ratio = isHoliday ? (meta.shortHopRatioHol || 0.5) : (meta.shortHopRatioWk || 0.45);
    const floor = isHoliday ? (meta.minShortHol || 6.1) : (meta.minShortWk || 3.7);
    const candNet = Number(Math.max(floor, upfrontFare * ratio).toFixed(1));
    if (candNet < upfrontFare - 0.5) {
      netFare = candNet;
      rebateAmount = Number((upfrontFare - netFare).toFixed(1));
      noticeEn = meta.noticeEn;
      noticeTc = meta.noticeTc;
    }
  } else if ((meta.rebateType === 'front_door_tap' || meta.rebateType === 'rear_door_tap') && alightStop) {
    const stopsRidden = (aSeq > bSeq && bSeq > 0) ? (aSeq - bSeq) : 99;
    const isShortHop = (hopKm <= 6.5 || stopsRidden <= 10);
    if (isShortHop && upfrontFare > (meta.shortHopNetFare || 6.5) + 0.5) {
      const targetNet = (hopKm <= 3.0 && meta.veryShortHopNetFare) ? meta.veryShortHopNetFare : meta.shortHopNetFare;
      netFare = Number(targetNet.toFixed(1));
      rebateAmount = Number((upfrontFare - netFare).toFixed(1));
      noticeEn = meta.noticeEn;
      noticeTc = meta.noticeTc;
    }
  }

  return {
    upfrontFare: Number(upfrontFare.toFixed(1)),
    netFare: Number(netFare.toFixed(1)),
    rebateAmount: Number(Math.max(0, rebateAmount).toFixed(1)),
    rebateType: rebateAmount > 0 ? meta.rebateType : null,
    noticeEn,
    noticeTc
  };
}
