function closeTutorialModal() { document.getElementById('tutorialModal').style.display = 'none'; }

function renderStopList() {
  const container = document.getElementById('stop-list-container');
  if (!container || !detailRouteGroup) return;
  const stops = getStopsForGroup(detailRouteGroup, detailTargetStopKey);
  const allowFinalStop = isCircularRoute(detailRouteGroup, stops);
  const isHol = isHongKongHolidayFareDay();
  const isPenultimateDualBranch = (detailRouteGroup.mode === 'MTR' && stops.length >= 3 && isParallelRailTerminusPair(stops[stops.length - 2]?.stopId, stops[stops.length - 1]?.stopId));
  const maxFareIdx = isPenultimateDualBranch ? stops.length - 3 : stops.length - 2;

  container.innerHTML = stops.map((s, idx) => {
    const stKey = s.stopKey || getGlobalStopKey(s);
    const bmId = `${detailRouteGroup.key}|${stKey}`;
    const isSaved = bookmarkedStops.some(b => b.id === bmId);
    const dStr = formatStopDistance(s);
    const fareProf = analyzeStopSectionFareProfile(detailRouteGroup, stops, idx, isHol);
    const fareHtml = (fareProf.fullFare > 0 && (idx <= maxFareIdx || allowFinalStop))
      ? `<span id="stop-fare-lbl-${idx}">${escapeHtml(fareProf.labelText)}</span>${fareProf.secBadge ? ` <span class="state-pill sched-ghost" style="padding:1px 6px;font-size:0.62rem;">${escapeHtml(fareProf.secBadge)}</span>` : ''}`
      : '';
    const hospBadge = isHospitalBranchStop(s) ? `<span class="state-pill">${t('viaHospitalTag')}</span>` : '';
    return `
      <div class="stop-item" id="detail-stop-item-${idx}" onclick="if(!isUserSelectingText())expandStop(${idx}, false, false, false)">
        <div class="stop-alight-col" id="stop-alight-col-${idx}"></div>
        <div class="stop-header-row">
          <div class="stop-title-group">
            <div class="stop-name"><span>${idx + 1}. ${escapeHtml(getStopName(s))}</span> ${hospBadge}</div>
            ${(fareHtml || dStr) ? `<div class="stop-fare-sub">${fareHtml}${dStr ? `<span class="location-dist-pill">· ${escapeHtml(dStr)}</span>` : ''}</div>` : ''}
          </div>
          <div class="stop-meta-right">
            <button class="stop-bm-btn ${isSaved ? 'saved' : ''}" id="stop-bm-btn-${idx}" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleStopBookmark(${idx});">${isSaved ? '★' : '☆'}</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="eta-expanded-container" id="detail-eta-expanded-${idx}"></div>
      </div>`;
  }).join('') + renderMtrStyleReportFooterHtml();
}

// ============================================================================
// SAVED BOOKMARKS VIEW
// ============================================================================
let favRenderToken = 0;

async function renderFavList(isSilentRefresh = false) {
  const listEl = document.getElementById('favList');
  if (!listEl) return;
  if (!routeMapIndex.size) initRouteMapFast();

  if (!bookmarkedStops.length) {
    listEl.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center">${t('noSavedRoutes')}</div>`;
    return;
  }

  if (!isSilentRefresh || !listEl.querySelector('.route-list-item')) {
    listEl.innerHTML = bookmarkedStops.map((bm, idx) => {
      const g = routeMapIndex.get(bm.groupKey);
      if (!g) return '';
      const dispBadge = window.getDisplayRouteBadge ? window.getDisplayRouteBadge(g) : g.routeNo;
      const stName = stripPlatformCodeFromDisplay(currentLang === 'zh' && bm.stopName_tc ? bm.stopName_tc : bm.stopName_e);
      return `
        <div class="route-list-item ${g.mode === 'FERRY' ? 'ferry-card' : ''}" onclick="if(!isUserSelectingText())openRouteDetail('${escapeJsAttr(g.key)}', '${escapeJsAttr(bm.stopKey)}')">
          <div class="route-no-col">
            <div class="route-no-text" style="color:${g.colorHex || 'var(--text-main)'};">${escapeHtml(dispBadge)}</div>
            <div class="route-co-tag">${escapeHtml(getCompanyName(g))}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="route-info-col">
            <div class="route-dest"><span class="to-prefix">${t('to')}</span>${escapeHtml(getRouteName(g))}</div>
            <div class="route-sub" id="fav-sub-${idx}"><span>${escapeHtml(stName)}</span></div>
          </div>
          <div class="route-eta-col" id="fav-eta-${idx}">
            <div class="eta-time" style="color:var(--text-sub);font-size:1.1rem;">···</div>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;margin-left:4px;" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
            <button class="fav-grip-btn" onclick="cycleFavBookmarkPosition(${idx})" title="Move">⇅</button>
            <button class="del-fav-btn" onclick="removeStopBookmark('${escapeJsAttr(bm.id)}')" title="Remove">✕</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  const myToken = ++favRenderToken;
  await Promise.all(bookmarkedStops.map(async (bm, idx) => {
    const g = routeMapIndex.get(bm.groupKey);
    if (!g) return;
    const arrs = await fetchEtaRaw(g, bm.stopKey, false, Date.now(), null, true);
    if (myToken !== favRenderToken || currentView !== 'fav') return;
    const etaEl = document.getElementById(`fav-eta-${idx}`);
    const subEl = document.getElementById(`fav-sub-${idx}`);
    if (!etaEl || !subEl) return;
    const stName = stripPlatformCodeFromDisplay(currentLang === 'zh' && bm.stopName_tc ? bm.stopName_tc : bm.stopName_e);
    if (arrs[0]) {
      const ui = formatEtaUI(arrs[0], g);
      const loadPillHtml = (g.mode !== 'FERRY' && arrs[0].loadEstimate)
        ? formatLoadPillHtml(arrs[0].loadEstimate, true)
        : '';
      etaEl.innerHTML = `<div class="eta-time">${ui.mainTimeStr}</div>${ui.clockStr ? `<div class="eta-clock-sub">${ui.clockStr}</div>` : ''}`;
      if (arrs[0].walkRedirect) {
        const wr = arrs[0].walkRedirect;
        const isZh = (currentLang === 'zh');
        const wrBadge = wr.savedMins > 0
          ? (isZh ? `💡 慳${wr.savedMins}分：行${wr.distM}m去 #${wr.stopIdx + 1} ${getStopName(wr)}` : `💡 Save ${wr.savedMins}m: Walk ${wr.distM}m ➔ #${wr.stopIdx + 1} ${getStopName(wr)}`)
          : (isZh ? `🚶 步行${wr.distM}m去 #${wr.stopIdx + 1} ${getStopName(wr)}` : `🚶 Walk ${wr.distM}m ➔ #${wr.stopIdx + 1} ${getStopName(wr)}`);
        subEl.innerHTML = `${loadPillHtml}<span style="color:var(--ios-blue);font-weight:600;">${escapeHtml(wrBadge)}</span>`;
      } else {
        subEl.innerHTML = `${loadPillHtml}<span>${escapeHtml(stName)}</span>`;
      }
    } else {
      etaEl.innerHTML = `<div class="eta-time" style="color:var(--text-sub);">-</div>`;
      subEl.innerHTML = `<span>${escapeHtml(stName)}</span>`;
    }
  }));
}

// ============================================================================
// STATUS & WATCHDOG MODAL
// ============================================================================
function auditLoadedTransitDatabase() {
  const EXPECTED_MTR = ['AEL', 'TCL', 'TML', 'TKL', 'EAL', 'SIL', 'TWL', 'ISL', 'KTL', 'DRL'];
  const EXPECTED_LRT = ['505', '507', '610', '614', '614P', '615', '615P', '705', '706', '751', '751P', '761P'];

  const presentMtr = new Set(groupedRoutes.filter(g => g.mode === 'MTR' && g.company === 'MTR').map(g => g.routeNo));
  const presentLrt = new Set(groupedRoutes.filter(g => g.mode === 'MTR' && g.company === 'LRT').map(g => g.routeNo));
  const missingMtr = EXPECTED_MTR.filter(x => !presentMtr.has(x));
  const missingLrt = EXPECTED_LRT.filter(x => !presentLrt.has(x));

  const ferryRoutes = groupedRoutes.filter(g => g.mode === 'FERRY');
  let ferryWithLiveKey = 0, ferryWithSched = 0, ferryDead = 0;

  ferryRoutes.forEach(g => {
    const lk = resolveFerryLiveEtaKeyClient(g.orig_e, g.dest_e) || g.liveEtaKey;
    const rStops = getStopsForGroup(g);
    const pairHdr = `${normStationLookupKey(g.orig_e)}_${normStationLookupKey(g.dest_e)}`;
    const pairStop = rStops.length >= 2
      ? `${normStationLookupKey(rStops[0].stopName_e)}_${normStationLookupKey(rStops[rStops.length - 1].stopName_e)}`
      : pairHdr;
    const isInterIslands = /pengchau|muiwo|chimawan|cheungchau/.test(pairStop);

    const sch = liveFerryApiCache.data?.csvSchedules?.[pairHdr]
      || liveFerryApiCache.data?.csvSchedules?.[pairStop]
      || g.ferryMeta?.sched
      || (isInterIslands ? liveFerryApiCache.data?.csvSchedules?.['pengchau_cheungchau'] : null);

    const hasSlots = sch && ((sch.wk?.length || 0) + (sch.sat?.length || 0) + (sch.hol?.length || 0) > 0);
    if (lk) ferryWithLiveKey++;
    if (hasSlots) ferryWithSched++;
    if (!lk && !hasSlots) ferryDead++;
  });

  let invalidCoordStops = 0;
  for (const g of groupedRoutes) {
    const rId = g.routeIds?.[0];
    if (!rId) continue;
    (routeStopsCache[rId] || []).forEach(s => {
      if (String(s?.stopId || '').startsWith('SYNTH_')) return;
      const la = parseFloat(s?.latitude), lo = parseFloat(s?.longitude);
      if (la === 0 && lo === 0) return;
      if (!isValidRailOrFerryCoord(la, lo)) invalidCoordStops++;
    });
  }

  const mtrMatrixExactTucLop = window.getExactMtrFare ? window.getExactMtrFare('TUC', 'LOP') : 0;
  const mtrEngineTucLop = typeof MTR_ENGINE !== 'undefined'
    ? MTR_ENGINE.lookupExactHeavyRailFare('TUC', 'LOP', window.getExactMtrFare)
    : 0;

  const issues = [];
  if (missingMtr.length) issues.push(`Missing MTR Lines: ${missingMtr.join(', ')}`);
  if (missingLrt.length) issues.push(`Missing LRT Routes: ${missingLrt.join(', ')}`);
  if (mtrMatrixExactTucLop <= 0) issues.push(`MTR O-D matrix pending v9 Action rebuild (using MTR_ENGINE calibrated fallback: TUC->LOP ≈ $${mtrEngineTucLop.toFixed(1)})`);
  if (!ferryRoutes.length) issues.push(`0 Ferry routes loaded!`);
  else if (ferryWithSched === 0 && ferryWithLiveKey === 0) issues.push(`Ferry Timetables Unhydrated: 0/${ferryRoutes.length} routes have matched schedules`);
  if (invalidCoordStops > 0) issues.push(`${invalidCoordStops} active stops have invalid/missing HK coordinates`);
  const edgeSt = SignalHealthMonitor.byOperator.EDGE;
  if (edgeSt.calls > 8) issues.push(`Edge Worker Loop Detected: ${edgeSt.calls} calls (${edgeSt.ms}ms last RTT)`);

  const hktTodayYmd = getHongKongDateYYYYMMDD();
  const reviewDate = typeof INTER_ISLANDS_REVIEW_AFTER !== 'undefined' ? INTER_ISLANDS_REVIEW_AFTER : '2027-01-10';
  const watchdogAlerts = [
    ...(Array.isArray(window.__DATA_DRIFT_ALERTS__) ? window.__DATA_DRIFT_ALERTS__ : []),
    ...Array.from((window.__LIVE_FERRY_DRIFT_ALERTS__ || new Map()).values())
  ];
  if (hktTodayYmd >= reviewDate && !watchdogAlerts.some(a => a.category === 'TEMP_WORKS_EXPIRY')) {
    watchdogAlerts.push({
      category: 'TEMP_WORKS_EXPIRY',
      date: hktTodayYmd,
      message: `Mui Wo Northern Berth 2.5-month temporary works window (${reviewDate}) has elapsed — check if Sun Ferry Inter-Islands has resumed normal schedule.`
    });
  }

  return {
    missingMtr, missingLrt,
    mtrMatrixExactTucLop, mtrEngineTucLop,
    ferryTotal: ferryRoutes.length, ferryWithLiveKey, ferryWithSched, ferryDead,
    invalidCoordStops, issues, watchdogAlerts
  };
}

async function openBuiltInStatusModal() {
  triggerHaptic(10);
  const modal = document.getElementById('builtInStatusModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'flex';

  const sumEl = document.getElementById('builtInStatusSummary');
  const rowsEl = document.getElementById('builtInStatusRows');
  const isZh = (currentLang === 'zh');

  const gpsSt = SignalHealthMonitor.getGpsStatus();
  const cellSt = SignalHealthMonitor.getCellStatus();
  const audit = auditLoadedTransitDatabase();

  const mtrCount = groupedRoutes.filter(g => g.mode === 'MTR' && g.company === 'MTR').length;
  const lrtCount = groupedRoutes.filter(g => g.mode === 'MTR' && g.company === 'LRT' && !/^(507P|610P)$/.test(g.routeNo)).length;
  const busCount = groupedRoutes.filter(g => !g.mode || g.mode === 'BUS').length;

  const overallHealthy = audit.issues.length === 0;
  const bannerStyle = overallHealthy
    ? 'background:rgba(52,199,89,0.14);border:1px solid rgba(52,199,89,0.45);color:var(--ios-green);'
    : 'background:rgba(255,149,0,0.14);border:1px solid rgba(255,149,0,0.45);color:var(--ios-orange);';

  const issuesHtml = audit.issues.length
    ? `<div style="${bannerStyle}border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin-top:7px;font-size:0.75rem;line-height:1.45;font-weight:700;">
         <div>⚠️ ${isZh ? `發現 ${audit.issues.length} 項數據提示：` : `${audit.issues.length} Notice(s) Detected:`}</div>
         ${audit.issues.map(x => `<div style="margin-top:2px;">• ${escapeHtml(x)}</div>`).join('')}
       </div>`
    : `<div style="${bannerStyle}border-radius:10px;padding:7px 10px;margin-top:7px;font-size:0.75rem;font-weight:700;">
         ✅ ${isZh ? `所有 10 條港鐵 (TUC➔LOP $${audit.mtrMatrixExactTucLop.toFixed(1)})、輕鐵、巴士及主線渡輪數據正常` : `All 10 MTR (TUC➔LOP $${audit.mtrMatrixExactTucLop.toFixed(1)}), LRT, Bus & Ferry tables verified`}
       </div>`;

  const reviewDate = typeof INTER_ISLANDS_REVIEW_AFTER !== 'undefined' ? INTER_ISLANDS_REVIEW_AFTER : '2027-01-10';
  const watchdogHtml = audit.watchdogAlerts.length > 0
    ? `<div style="background:rgba(255,149,0,0.15);border:1px solid rgba(255,149,0,0.45);color:var(--ios-orange);border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin-top:7px;font-size:0.74rem;line-height:1.45;font-weight:700;">
         <div>🔔 ${isZh ? `車資及時間表變動監察 (${audit.watchdogAlerts.length} 項)：` : `Schedule & Fare Drift Watchdog (${audit.watchdogAlerts.length} Notice):`}</div>
         ${audit.watchdogAlerts.map(a => `<div style="margin-top:3px;">• [${escapeHtml(a.category)}] ${escapeHtml(a.message)}</div>`).join('')}
       </div>`
    : `<div style="background:var(--bg-surface);border:1px solid var(--border-color);border-radius:10px;padding:7px 10px;margin-top:7px;font-size:0.72rem;color:var(--text-sub);line-height:1.4;">
         <div>🛡️ <strong>${isZh ? '自動監察運作正常' : 'Fare & Schedule Drift Watchdog Active'}</strong>: ${isZh ? '每週自動比對港鐵車資表、輕鐵及渡輪 CSV，如有變動會自動於 GitHub 發出電郵通知。' : 'Weekly Action & Live API canary monitor MTR fares, LRT CSVs and Ferry schedules (emails on drift).'}</div>
         <div style="margin-top:3px;color:var(--ios-orange);">🛠️ ${isZh ? `生效中臨時調整：橫水渡梅窩北面泊位工程時間表 (將於 ${reviewDate} 提醒覆核)` : `Active Temp Schedule: Inter-Islands Mui Wo N. Berth works (review reminder set for ${reviewDate})`}</div>
       </div>`;

  if (sumEl) {
    sumEl.innerHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex; gap:6px; flex-wrap:wrap; align-items:center;">
        <span class="state-pill ${gpsSt.grade === 'GOOD' ? 'live-gps' : 'sched-ghost'}">🎯 GPS: ${escapeHtml(gpsSt.label)}</span>
        <span class="state-pill ${cellSt.grade === 'GOOD' ? 'live-gps' : 'sched-ghost'}">📶 Net: ${escapeHtml(cellSt.label)}</span>
        <button class="header-pill-btn gps-reset-pill" onclick="runLiveTruthProbe()" style="height:26px;padding:0 9px;font-size:0.7rem;">🔬 ${isZh ? '實時抽樣及對照' : 'Run Live Probe'}</button>
      </div>
      <div style="font-size:0.73rem; color:var(--text-sub); margin-top:6px; line-height:1.4;">
        🚌 Bus: <strong>${busCount}</strong> •
        🚇 MTR: <strong style="color:${mtrCount >= 20 ? 'var(--ios-green)' : 'var(--ios-red)'}">${mtrCount}/20</strong> •
        🚋 LRT: <strong style="color:${lrtCount >= 24 ? 'var(--ios-green)' : 'var(--ios-red)'}">${lrtCount}/24</strong> (69 Stops) •
        ⛴️ Ferry: <strong>${audit.ferryTotal}</strong> (Live:${audit.ferryWithLiveKey} / Sched:<strong style="color:${audit.ferryWithSched > 0 ? 'var(--ios-green)' : 'var(--ios-red)'}">${audit.ferryWithSched}</strong>)
      </div>
      ${issuesHtml}
      ${watchdogHtml}`;
  }

  if (rowsEl) {
    const opLabels = {
      KMB:   'KMB / LWB (etabus.gov.hk)',
      CTB:   'Citybus (rt.data.gov.hk)',
      NLB:   'New Lantao Bus (rt.data.gov.hk)',
      GMB:   'Green Minibus (etagmb.gov.hk)',
      MTR:   'MTR Heavy Rail (getSchedule)',
      LRT:   'Light Rail (lrt/getSchedule)',
      FERRY: 'Ferry Live + Timetable Yield',
      EDGE:  'Cloudflare Edge (/api/worker)'
    };

    rowsEl.innerHTML = Object.entries(opLabels).map(([k, title]) => {
      const st = SignalHealthMonitor.byOperator[k] || { calls: 0, ms: 0, errors: 0, emptyPayloads: 0, lastYieldCount: null };
      let cls = 'district-tag', txt = isZh ? '未呼叫' : 'Untested';

      if (st.calls > 0) {
        const errRate = st.errors / st.calls;
        const isLoopingEdge = (k === 'EDGE' && st.calls > 8);
        const isZeroYield = (st.lastYieldCount === 0 && st.emptyPayloads >= 1);
        const isSlow = st.ms > 2000;

        if (errRate >= 0.35 || isLoopingEdge) {
          cls = 'load-full';
          txt = isLoopingEdge ? `LOOP (${st.calls} req • ${st.ms}ms)` : `ERR ${st.errors}/${st.calls} (${st.ms}ms)`;
        } else if (isZeroYield) {
          cls = 'sched-ghost';
          txt = `0 ETAs (${st.ms}ms • ${st.calls} req)`;
        } else if (isSlow || st.errors > 0) {
          cls = 'sched-ghost';
          txt = `${st.ms}ms (${st.calls} req)`;
        } else {
          cls = 'live-gps';
          const yieldTag = st.lastYieldCount !== null ? ` • ${st.lastYieldCount} ETA✓` : '';
          txt = `${st.ms}ms (${st.calls} req${yieldTag})`;
        }
      }

      return `<div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; gap:6px; padding:7px 10px; background:var(--bg-surface); border-radius:10px;">
        <span style="font-size:0.77rem;">${escapeHtml(title)}</span>
        <span class="state-pill ${cls}">${escapeHtml(txt)}</span>
      </div>`;
    }).join('');
  }
}

function closeBuiltInStatusModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('builtInStatusModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

async function runLiveTruthProbe() {
  triggerHaptic(14);
  showToast(currentLang === 'zh' ? '🔬 正在實測港鐵、輕鐵、巴士及渡輪時間表對照...' : '🔬 Probing live MTR, LRT, Bus & Ferry schedules...');
  const samples = [
    groupedRoutes.find(g => g.mode === 'MTR' && g.routeNo === 'TCL'),
    groupedRoutes.find(g => g.mode === 'MTR' && g.company === 'LRT' && g.routeNo === '505') || groupedRoutes.find(g => g.mode === 'MTR' && g.company === 'LRT'),
    groupedRoutes.find(g => g.company === 'KMB'),
    groupedRoutes.find(g => g.company === 'NLB' && g.routeNo === '3M'),
    groupedRoutes.find(g => g.mode === 'FERRY' && /mui wo/i.test(g.orig_e) && /central/i.test(g.dest_e)),
    groupedRoutes.find(g => g.mode === 'FERRY' && /peng chau/i.test(g.orig_e) && /mui wo|cheung chau/i.test(g.dest_e))
  ].filter(Boolean);

  await Promise.all(samples.map(async (g) => {
    const stops = getStopsForGroup(g);
    const s0 = g.company === 'LRT'
      ? (stops.find((s, idx) => idx < stops.length - 1 && resolveLrtNumericId(s.lrtCode || s.stopId, s.stopName_e, s.name_tc)) || stops[0])
      : stops[0];
    if (s0) await fetchStopSingleRaw(g, s0, true);
  }));
  syncSettingsModalLabels();
  openBuiltInStatusModal();
}

function applyLanguage() {
  document.title = t('docTitle');
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-i18n]').forEach(el => {
    const k = el.getAttribute('data-i18n');
    if (k) el.innerText = t(k);
  });
  const sInp = document.getElementById('stopSearchInput');
  if (sInp) sInp.placeholder = t('searchStopPlaceholder');
  const mapTitle = document.getElementById('mapBrowserHeaderTitle');
  if (mapTitle) mapTitle.innerText = t('mapTapHint');

  syncSettingsModalLabels();
  updateTopBackButtonVisibility();
  updateCommuteBarUI();
  updateMainStopHeaderDisplay();

  if (currentView === 'main') renderMainNearby(false);
  else if (currentView === 'detail' && detailRouteGroup) {
    updateKmbDetailHeader();
    renderStopList();
    if (detailExpandedStopIdx >= 0) expandStop(detailExpandedStopIdx, true, false, true);
  } else if (currentView === 'xfer') renderTransferPlannerView();
  else if (currentView === 'fav') renderFavList(false);
}

// ============================================================================
// MAINLINE FERRY LINKER (Uses VERIFIED_MAINLINE_FERRIES from hardcode.js)
// ============================================================================
function isMainlineFerryRoute(g) {
  if (!g || g.mode !== 'FERRY') return true;
  const o = normStationLookupKey(g.orig_e);
  const d = normStationLookupKey(g.dest_e);
  const pair = `${o}_${d}`;
  if (typeof VERIFIED_MAINLINE_FERRIES !== 'undefined' && VERIFIED_MAINLINE_FERRIES[pair]) return true;
  if ((o === 'chimawan' || d === 'chimawan') && (o === 'muiwo' || d === 'muiwo' || o === 'pengchau' || d === 'pengchau' || o === 'cheungchau' || d === 'cheungchau')) {
    return true;
  }
  if ((o === 'shalowan' || d === 'shalowan') && (o === 'tuenmun' || d === 'tuenmun' || o === 'tungchung' || d === 'tungchung' || o === 'taio' || d === 'taio')) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function mergeVerifiedMainlineSchedules(targetObj = {}) {
  const out = { ...targetObj };
  if (typeof VERIFIED_MAINLINE_FERRIES === 'undefined') return out;
  for (const [pairKey, spec] of Object.entries(VERIFIED_MAINLINE_FERRIES)) {
    out[pairKey] = {
      wk: spec.wk,
      sat: spec.sat,
      hol: spec.hol,
      updated: spec.updated,
      ...(spec.tempNoticeEn ? { tempNoticeEn: spec.tempNoticeEn, tempNoticeTc: spec.tempNoticeTc, reviewAfter: spec.reviewAfter } : {})
    };
  }
  return out;
}

function enrichFerryRouteFromDynamicMeta(g) {
  if (!g) return g;
  if (!Array.isArray(g.activeWindows) || !g.activeWindows.length) {
    g.activeWindows = window.__DERIVE_DEFAULT_ACTIVE_WINDOWS__(g);
  }
  if (g.mode !== 'FERRY') return g;
  const fixedKey = resolveFerryLiveEtaKeyClient(g.orig_e, g.dest_e);
  if (fixedKey) g.liveEtaKey = fixedKey;

  const dirKey = `${normStationLookupKey(g.orig_e)}_${normStationLookupKey(g.dest_e)}`;
  const vSpec = typeof VERIFIED_MAINLINE_FERRIES !== 'undefined' ? VERIFIED_MAINLINE_FERRIES[dirKey] : null;
  if (!g.ferryMeta) g.ferryMeta = {};

  if (vSpec) {
    g.ferryMeta.dur         = vSpec.dur;
    g.ferryMeta.durFast     = vSpec.durFast;
    g.ferryMeta.durOrd      = vSpec.durOrd;
    g.ferryMeta.fareWk      = vSpec.fareWk;
    g.ferryMeta.fareHol     = vSpec.fareHol;
    g.ferryMeta.fareWkOrd   = vSpec.fareWkOrd;
    g.ferryMeta.fareWkFast  = vSpec.fareWkFast;
    g.ferryMeta.fareHolOrd  = vSpec.fareHolOrd;
    g.ferryMeta.fareHolFast = vSpec.fareHolFast;
    if (vSpec.tempNoticeEn) {
      g.ferryMeta.tempNoticeEn = vSpec.tempNoticeEn;
      g.ferryMeta.tempNoticeTc = vSpec.tempNoticeTc;
      g.ferryMeta.reviewAfter  = vSpec.reviewAfter;
    }
    g.ferryMeta.sched = {
      wk: vSpec.wk,
      sat: vSpec.sat,
      hol: vSpec.hol,
      updated: vSpec.updated,
      ...(vSpec.tempNoticeEn ? { tempNoticeEn: vSpec.tempNoticeEn, tempNoticeTc: vSpec.tempNoticeTc, reviewAfter: vSpec.reviewAfter } : {})
    };
  }

  const wkFare = g.ferryMeta.fareWkFast || g.ferryMeta.fareWk || 0;
  const holFare = g.ferryMeta.fareHolFast || g.ferryMeta.fareHol || wkFare;
  if (wkFare > 0) {
    (g.routeIds || []).forEach(rId => {
      (routeStopsCache[rId] || []).forEach(st => {
        st.fare = String(wkFare);
        st.fareHol = String(holFare);
      });
    });
  }
  return g;
}

function filterToMainlineFerriesOnly() {
  groupedRoutes = groupedRoutes.filter(isMainlineFerryRoute).map(enrichFerryRouteFromDynamicMeta);
  const validFerryFullKeys = new Set(groupedRoutes.filter(g => g.mode === 'FERRY').map(g => g.key));
  const validFerryBaseKeys = new Set(groupedRoutes.filter(g => g.mode === 'FERRY').map(g => String(g.key).replace(/_(O|I)$/, '')));
  if (Array.isArray(window.QUICK_FERRY_LINES)) {
    window.QUICK_FERRY_LINES = window.QUICK_FERRY_LINES.filter(f => validFerryBaseKeys.has(f.key));
  }
  if (Array.isArray(window.MULTI_MODAL_HUBS_DIRECTORY)) {
    window.MULTI_MODAL_HUBS_DIRECTORY = window.MULTI_MODAL_HUBS_DIRECTORY.filter(h => h.mode !== 'FERRY' || validFerryFullKeys.has(h.groupKey));
  }
}

let _lastCorridorsSig = '';
async function syncEdgeTrafficCorridors() {
  if (document.hidden) return;
  try {
    const d = await fetchEdgeApiJSON('', 3500, false);
    if (!d?.ok) return;

    if (d.highwayCorridors && typeof d.highwayCorridors === 'object') {
      const nextSig = Object.entries(d.highwayCorridors)
        .filter(([k]) => k !== 'TM_CLK_TUNNEL')
        .map(([k, v]) => `${k}:${v?.status || 'SMOOTH'}`)
        .join('|');
      window.__LIVE_CORRIDORS__ = d.highwayCorridors;
      window.__LIVE_HIGHWAY_KMH__ = Number(d.highwayCorridors.LANTAU_TM?.speedKmh || d.highwayCorridors.TM_CLK_TUNNEL?.speedKmh) || 70;
      if (_lastCorridorsSig && _lastCorridorsSig !== nextSig && typeof window.invalidateRaptorGraph === 'function') {
        window.invalidateRaptorGraph();
      }
      _lastCorridorsSig = nextSig;
    }

    if (d.weather && typeof d.weather === 'object') {
      window.__HKO_WEATHER__ = d.weather;
    }

    if (typeof syncRelevantCorridorPillUI === 'function') {
      syncRelevantCorridorPillUI();
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

async function executeSync(forceManual = false) {
  if (document.hidden && !forceManual) return;
  if (isSyncing && !forceManual) return;
  isSyncing = true;
  const dot = document.getElementById('bgSyncDot');
  dot?.classList.add('syncing');

  try {
    if (currentView === 'main' && !isStopSearchOpen()) {
      await renderMainNearby(true);
    } else if (currentView === 'detail' && detailRouteGroup && detailExpandedStopIdx >= 0) {
      await expandStop(detailExpandedStopIdx, true, false, true);
    } else if (currentView === 'fav') {
      await renderFavList(true);
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  setTimeout(() => dot?.classList.remove('syncing'), 280);
  isSyncing = false;
}

window.openCompanionAppAtCurrentStop = function(target) {
  let st = null;
  if (currentView === 'detail' && detailRouteGroup) {
    const rStops = getStopsForGroup(detailRouteGroup, detailTargetStopKey);
    st = rStops[detailExpandedStopIdx >= 0 ? detailExpandedStopIdx : 0];
  }
  if (!st) {
    st = stopMapIndex.get(currentNearestStopKey) || nearbyStopsList[0];
  }
  const lat = st ? parseFloat(st.latitude) : (lastKnownPos?.lat || 22.2819);
  const lon = st ? parseFloat(st.longitude) : (lastKnownPos?.lon || 114.1556);
  if (target === 'property') {
    window.open(`https://hkproperty.pages.dev/#lat=${lat.toFixed(5)}&lng=${lon.toFixed(5)}&from=transit`, '_blank', 'noopener');
  } else {
    window.open(`https://hkpast.pages.dev/#1889-victoria/16/${lat.toFixed(4)}/${lon.toFixed(4)}/hk/split`, '_blank', 'noopener');
  }
};

(async function boot() {
  BlackboxLogger.init();
  applyLanguage();

  if (!liveFerryApiCache.data) {
    liveFerryApiCache.data = { liveEtas: {}, csvSchedules: {}, keyTs: {} };
  }
  liveFerryApiCache.data.csvSchedules = mergeVerifiedMainlineSchedules(liveFerryApiCache.data.csvSchedules);

  const [earlyIdx, earlyGps] = await Promise.all([
    window.__EARLY_INDEX_PROMISE__,
    window.__EARLY_GPS_PROMISE__
  ]);

  if (earlyGps && Number.isFinite(earlyGps.lat) && earlyGps.acc <= 450) {
    SignalHealthMonitor.recordGpsFix(earlyGps.acc);
    lastKnownPos = { lat: earlyGps.lat, lon: earlyGps.lon, acc: Math.round(earlyGps.acc), hasLiveFix: true };
    SafeStorage.setItem('transit_last_gps_pos_v2', JSON.stringify(lastKnownPos));
  }

  if (earlyIdx?.data) {
    const db = earlyIdx.data;
    routeStopsCache = db.routeStopsCache || {};
    groupedRoutes = Array.isArray(db.groupedRoutes) ? db.groupedRoutes : [];
    window.__LAST_TD_UPDATED__ = db.tdLastUpdated || db.lastUpdated || '';
    if (Array.isArray(db.dataDriftAlerts)) {
      window.__DATA_DRIFT_ALERTS__ = db.dataDriftAlerts;
    }
    window.MULTI_MODAL_HUBS_DIRECTORY = Array.isArray(db.multiModalHubs) ? db.multiModalHubs : [];
    window.MTR_EXITS_DIRECTORY = Array.isArray(db.mtrExits) ? db.mtrExits : [];
    window.QUICK_MTR_LINES = (db.quickMtrLines || []).map(x => ({ ...x, color: window.__GET_ROUTE_COLOR_HEX__('MTR', 'MTR', x.key || x.badge) }));
    window.QUICK_LRT_LINES = (db.quickLrtLines || []).map(x => ({ ...x, color: '#5856D6' }));
    window.QUICK_FERRY_LINES = (db.quickFerryLines || []).map(x => ({ ...x, color: '#0090D9' }));
    liveFerryApiCache.data.csvSchedules = mergeVerifiedMainlineSchedules(db.csvSchedules || {});
    filterToMainlineFerriesOnly();
    initRouteMapFast();
    syncSettingsModalLabels();
  }

  const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search || window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, ''));
  const extLat = parseFloat(urlParams.get('lat'));
  const extLng = parseFloat(urlParams.get('lng') || urlParams.get('lon'));
  const extZoom = parseInt(urlParams.get('z'), 10) || 16;
  const openMapTab = urlParams.get('view') === 'map' || Boolean(urlParams.get('from'));

  if (isValidRailOrFerryCoord(extLat, extLng)) {
    lastKnownPos = { lat: extLat, lon: extLng, acc: 15, hasLiveFix: true };
    userManualStopBrowseUntilMs = Date.now() + 600000;
    syncNearestStopFromGPS(extLat, extLng, true);
    renderMainNearby(false);
    if (openMapTab) {
      switchView('map', true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (window.browseMapInstance) {
          window.browseMapInstance.setView([extLat, extLng], Math.min(18, Math.max(14, extZoom)), { animate: false });
          renderBrowseMapVisiblePins();
          if (browseMapClickedSpotMarker) {
            try { window.browseMapInstance.removeLayer(browseMapClickedSpotMarker); } catch (e) {}
          }
          browseMapClickedSpotMarker = L.circleMarker([extLat, extLng], {
            radius: 9, fillColor: '#FF9500', color: '#FFFFFF', weight: 2.5, fillOpacity: 0.95
          }).addTo(window.browseMapInstance);
          browseMapClickedSpotMarker.bindPopup(buildBrowseMapActionPopupHtml({
            stopKey: `EXT_PIN_${extLat.toFixed(5)}_${extLng.toFixed(5)}`,
            nameEn: `Linked Spot (${extLat.toFixed(4)}, ${extLng.toFixed(4)})`,
            nameTc: `跨平台連結位置 (${extLat.toFixed(4)}, ${extLng.toFixed(4)})`,
            lat: extLat, lon: extLng, nodeType: 'ADDRESS', hasStopRadar: false
          }), { closeButton: true, offset: [0, -4] }).openPopup();
        }
      }, 95);
    }
  } else {
    syncNearestStopFromGPS(lastKnownPos.lat, lastKnownPos.lon, true);
    renderMainNearby(false);
    autoAcquireLiveGpsAndSnap(false);
  }

  setTimeout(() => {
    syncEdgeTrafficCorridors();
  }, 350);

  window.addEventListener('pageshow', (ev) => {
    if (ev.persisted || allStops.length === 0) {
      executeSync(true);
    }
  });

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    if (!document.hidden) {
      tickLiveEtasInDOM();
      countdown = getAdaptiveRefreshIntervalSec();
      executeSync(false);
    }
  });

  countdown = getAdaptiveRefreshIntervalSec();
  setInterval(() => {
    if (document.hidden) return;
    countdown--;
    if (countdown <= 0) {
      countdown = getAdaptiveRefreshIntervalSec();
      executeSync(false);
    }
  }, 1000);

  setInterval(syncEdgeTrafficCorridors, 60000);
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
```

// ============================================================================
// File: map.js (johntjz/Hk-Fleet — Unified Leaflet Geometry & Map Engine)
// Patch: 2026-11-20 22:25 HKT — Async OSRM Street-Aware Cycling Polylines + Pin Separation

window.mapInstance = null;
window.browseMapInstance = null;
window.xferMapInstance = null;

var detailPolylineLayer = null;
var mapMarkers = {};
var currentDetailRoadCoords = [];
var detailMapHeightState = 0;
var isDetailGpsLocked = false;

var userCompassHeadingDeg = null;
var isCompassListening = false;
var userMarkerDetail = null;
var userMarkerBrowse = null;
var userAccCircleBrowse = null;

var browseMapStopGrid = new Map();
var browseMapExitGrid = new Map();
var activeBrowseMarkersByKey = new Map();
var browseMapMoveDebounceTimer = null;
var browseMapClickedSpotMarker = null;

var xferMapLayers = [];
var isXferMapExpanded = false;
var xferDrawToken = 0;
var bikeRouteGeoCache = new Map();
var _mapResizeObservers = new WeakMap();

window.attachStandardMapLayers = function(targetMap) {
  if (!targetMap || typeof L === 'undefined') return;
  L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
    maxNativeZoom: 18,
    maxZoom: 19,
    keepBuffer: 6,
    updateWhenIdle: false,
    className: 'dark-tiles',
    attribution: '&copy; OSM'
  }).addTo(targetMap);
};

function attachMapResizeObserver(containerId, mapInst, onVisibleResize = null) {
  if (typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined' || !mapInst) return;
  const el = document.getElementById(containerId);
  if (!el || _mapResizeObservers.has(el)) return;
  let lastW = 0, lastH = 0;
  const ro = new ResizeObserver((entries) => {
    const rect = entries[0]?.contentRect;
    if (!rect || rect.width < 40 || rect.height < 40) return;
    const w = Math.round(rect.width), h = Math.round(rect.height);
    if (w === lastW && h === lastH) return;
    const wasHidden = (lastW === 0 || lastH === 0);
    lastW = w;
    lastH = h;
    try { mapInst.invalidateSize(false); } catch (e) {}
    if (wasHidden && typeof onVisibleResize === 'function') {
      onVisibleResize();
    }
  });
  ro.observe(el);
  _mapResizeObservers.set(el, ro);
}

// ============================================================================
// 1. GPS USER MARKER & COMPASS CONE
// ============================================================================
function buildGpsUserHeadingIconHtml(headingDeg) {
  const hasHdg = Number.isFinite(headingDeg);
  const rot = hasHdg ? Math.round(headingDeg) : 0;
  const beamSvg = hasHdg
    ? `<svg class="gps-user-beam" viewBox="0 0 44 44" style="transform:rotate(${rot}deg);"><path d="M22 22 L10 3 A20 20 0 0 1 34 3 Z" fill="rgba(0,122,255,0.32)"/></svg>`
    : '';
  return `<div class="gps-user-dot-wrap">${beamSvg}<div class="gps-user-core"></div></div>`;
}

function updateUserMarkers() {
  if (typeof L === 'undefined' || typeof lastKnownPos === 'undefined' || !lastKnownPos || !Number.isFinite(lastKnownPos.lat)) return;
  const lat = lastKnownPos.lat, lon = lastKnownPos.lon;
  const acc = Math.min(250, Math.max(12, Number(lastKnownPos.acc) || 25));

  if (window.mapInstance) {
    if (!userMarkerDetail) {
      userMarkerDetail = L.marker([lat, lon], {
        icon: L.divIcon({ className: '', html: buildGpsUserHeadingIconHtml(userCompassHeadingDeg), iconSize: [44, 44], iconAnchor: [22, 22] }),
        zIndexOffset: 900
      }).addTo(window.mapInstance);
    } else {
      userMarkerDetail.setLatLng([lat, lon]);
      userMarkerDetail.setIcon(L.divIcon({ className: '', html: buildGpsUserHeadingIconHtml(userCompassHeadingDeg), iconSize: [44, 44], iconAnchor: [22, 22] }));
    }
    if (isDetailGpsLocked) {
      window.mapInstance.panTo([lat, lon], { animate: true, duration: 0.3 });
    }
  }

  if (window.browseMapInstance) {
    if (!userAccCircleBrowse) {
      userAccCircleBrowse = L.circle([lat, lon], {
        radius: acc, color: '#007AFF', weight: 1, fillColor: '#007AFF', fillOpacity: 0.14, interactive: false
      }).addTo(window.browseMapInstance);
    } else {
      userAccCircleBrowse.setLatLng([lat, lon]);
      userAccCircleBrowse.setRadius(acc);
    }

    const isZh = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'zh');
    const hdgStr = Number.isFinite(userCompassHeadingDeg) ? ` • 🧭 ${Math.round(userCompassHeadingDeg)}°` : '';
    const tipTxt = isZh
      ? `📍 我的位置${lastKnownPos.hasLiveFix ? ` (±${Math.round(acc)}m${hdgStr})` : ' (最後位置)'}`
      : `📍 My Location${lastKnownPos.hasLiveFix ? ` (±${Math.round(acc)}m${hdgStr})` : ' (Last Known)'}`;

    if (!userMarkerBrowse) {
      userMarkerBrowse = L.marker([lat, lon], {
        icon: L.divIcon({ className: '', html: buildGpsUserHeadingIconHtml(userCompassHeadingDeg), iconSize: [44, 44], iconAnchor: [22, 22] }),
        zIndexOffset: 950
      }).addTo(window.browseMapInstance);
      userMarkerBrowse.bindTooltip(tipTxt, { direction: 'top', offset: [0, -10], className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
    } else {
      userMarkerBrowse.setLatLng([lat, lon]);
      userMarkerBrowse.setIcon(L.divIcon({ className: '', html: buildGpsUserHeadingIconHtml(userCompassHeadingDeg), iconSize: [44, 44], iconAnchor: [22, 22] }));
      userMarkerBrowse.setTooltipContent(tipTxt);
    }
  }
}

function handleDeviceOrientationEvent(ev) {
  let hdg = null;
  if (typeof ev.webkitCompassHeading === 'number' && Number.isFinite(ev.webkitCompassHeading)) {
    hdg = ev.webkitCompassHeading;
  } else if (typeof ev.alpha === 'number' && Number.isFinite(ev.alpha)) {
    hdg = (360 - ev.alpha) % 360;
  }
  if (hdg === null) return;
  userCompassHeadingDeg = (hdg + 360) % 360;
  updateUserMarkers();
}

async function autoStartCompassCone(fromUserGesture = false) {
  if (isCompassListening) return;
  try {
    if (typeof DeviceOrientationEvent !== 'undefined' && typeof DeviceOrientationEvent.requestPermission === 'function') {
      if (!fromUserGesture) return;
      const perm = await DeviceOrientationEvent.requestPermission().catch(() => '');
      if (perm !== 'granted') return;
    }
    isCompassListening = true;
    window.addEventListener('deviceorientationabsolute', handleDeviceOrientationEvent, true);
    window.addEventListener('deviceorientation', handleDeviceOrientationEvent, true);
  } catch (e) {}
}

// ============================================================================
// 2. SHARED STOP-ID SLICER, CURVED WALK GUIDES & OSRM CYCLING ROUTER
// ============================================================================
function getStopLatLon(st) {
  if (!st) return null;
  const lat = parseFloat(st.lat ?? st.latitude);
  const lon = parseFloat(st.lon ?? st.longitude);
  if (!isValidRailOrFerryCoord(lat, lon)) return null;
  return [lat, lon];
}

function matchStopIndexInSequence(stops, targetStop, minIdx = 0) {
  if (!Array.isArray(stops) || !stops.length || !targetStop) return -1;
  const tCode = String(targetStop.mtrCode || targetStop.lrtCode || targetStop.stopId || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const tKey = String(targetStop.stopKey || '');
  const tSeq = Number(targetStop.seq || 0);
  const tNorm = typeof normStationLookupKey === 'function' ? normStationLookupKey(targetStop.stopName_e) : '';
  const tBase = typeof getBaseStopNameKey === 'function' ? getBaseStopNameKey(targetStop.stopName_e) : '';
  const tPt = getStopLatLon(targetStop);

  if (tSeq > 0 && tCode) {
    const idx = stops.findIndex((s, i) =>
      i >= minIdx &&
      Number(s.seq || (i + 1)) === tSeq &&
      String(s.mtrCode || s.lrtCode || s.stopId || '').trim().toUpperCase() === tCode
    );
    if (idx !== -1) return idx;
  }

  if (tCode) {
    const idx = stops.findIndex((s, i) =>
      i >= minIdx &&
      String(s.mtrCode || s.lrtCode || s.stopId || '').trim().toUpperCase() === tCode
    );
    if (idx !== -1) return idx;
  }

  if (tKey) {
    const idx = stops.findIndex((s, i) => i >= minIdx && s.stopKey === tKey);
    if (idx !== -1) return idx;
  }

  if (tNorm || tBase) {
    const idx = stops.findIndex((s, i) => {
      if (i < minIdx) return false;
      const sBase = typeof getBaseStopNameKey === 'function' ? getBaseStopNameKey(s.stopName_e) : '';
      if (tBase && sBase === tBase) return true;
      const sNorm = typeof normStationLookupKey === 'function' ? normStationLookupKey(s.stopName_e) : '';
      if (tNorm && sNorm && sNorm === tNorm) {
        if (!tPt) return true;
        const sPt = getStopLatLon(s);
        return sPt ? calculateDistanceKm(tPt[0], tPt[1], sPt[0], sPt[1]) <= 0.45 : true;
      }
      return false;
    });
    if (idx !== -1) return idx;
  }

  if (tPt) {
    let bestIdx = -1, bestKm = 0.35;
    for (let i = minIdx; i < stops.length; i++) {
      const sPt = getStopLatLon(stops[i]);
      if (!sPt) continue;
      const dKm = calculateDistanceKm(tPt[0], tPt[1], sPt[0], sPt[1]);
      if (dKm < bestKm) {
        bestKm = dKm;
        bestIdx = i;
      }
    }
    if (bestIdx !== -1) return bestIdx;
  }

  return -1;
}

function sliceRouteStopPoints(group, bStop, aStop) {
  const bPt = getStopLatLon(bStop);
  const aPt = getStopLatLon(aStop);
  const directFallback = [
    bPt ? { pt: bPt, stop: bStop } : null,
    aPt ? { pt: aPt, stop: aStop } : null
  ].filter(Boolean);

  if (!group || !bStop || !aStop) return directFallback;

  const bCode = String(bStop?.mtrCode || bStop?.lrtCode || bStop?.stopId || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const aCode = String(aStop?.mtrCode || aStop?.lrtCode || aStop?.stopId || '').trim().toUpperCase();

  let stops = getStopsForGroup(group, bStop.stopKey);
  if (group.mode === 'MTR') {
    stops = stops.filter(s => {
      const sc = String(s.mtrCode || s.lrtCode || s.stopId || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (sc === bCode || sc === aCode) return true;
      return !isParallelRailTerminusPair(sc, bCode) && !isParallelRailTerminusPair(sc, aCode);
    });
  }
  if (!stops.length) return directFallback;

  const bIdx = matchStopIndexInSequence(stops, bStop, 0);
  if (bIdx === -1) return directFallback;

  const aIdx = matchStopIndexInSequence(stops, aStop, bIdx + 1);
  if (aIdx === -1 || aIdx <= bIdx) {
    return directFallback;
  }

  const slice = stops
    .slice(bIdx, aIdx + 1)
    .map(s => {
      const pt = getStopLatLon(s);
      return pt ? { pt, stop: s } : null;
    })
    .filter(Boolean);

  return slice.length >= 2 ? slice : directFallback;
}

function sliceRouteStops(group, bStop, aStop) {
  return sliceRouteStopPoints(group, bStop, aStop).map(x => x.pt);
}

function buildGentleCurvePoints(fromPt, toPt, steps = 14) {
  const lat1 = fromPt[0], lon1 = fromPt[1];
  const lat2 = toPt[0], lon2 = toPt[1];
  const dLat = lat2 - lat1, dLon = lon2 - lon1;
  const midLat = (lat1 + lat2) / 2;
  const midLon = (lon1 + lon2) / 2;
  const ctrlLat = midLat - dLon * 0.11;
  const ctrlLon = midLon + dLat * 0.11;

  const pts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    const t = i / steps;
    const omt = 1 - t;
    pts.push([
      omt * omt * lat1 + 2 * omt * t * ctrlLat + t * t * lat2,
      omt * omt * lon1 + 2 * omt * t * ctrlLon + t * t * lon2
    ]);
  }
  return pts;
}

async function fetchOsrmBikeGeometry(fromPt, toPt) {
  if (!fromPt || !toPt) return null;
  const key = `${fromPt[0].toFixed(4)},${fromPt[1].toFixed(4)}_${toPt[0].toFixed(4)},${toPt[1].toFixed(4)}`;
  if (bikeRouteGeoCache.has(key)) return bikeRouteGeoCache.get(key);

  if (bikeRouteGeoCache.size > 60) {
    const firstKey = bikeRouteGeoCache.keys().next().value;
    if (firstKey) bikeRouteGeoCache.delete(firstKey);
  }

  const url = `https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/cycling/${fromPt[1].toFixed(6)},${fromPt[0].toFixed(6)};${toPt[1].toFixed(6)},${toPt[0].toFixed(6)}?overview=full&geometries=geojson`;
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const tid = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), 3000);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: ctrl.signal });
    clearTimeout(tid);
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const data = await res.json();
    const rt = data?.routes?.[0];
    const rawCoords = rt?.geometry?.coordinates;
    if (!Array.isArray(rawCoords) || rawCoords.length < 2) return null;

    // Flip GeoJSON [lon, lat] to Leaflet [lat, lon]
    const latLngs = rawCoords.map(([lng, lat]) => [lat, lng]);
    const distM = Math.round(Number(rt.distance) || 0);
    // Use realistic HK urban cycling speed (~15 km/h = 4.16 m/s) + 90s curbside unlock
    const bikeMins = Math.max(2, Math.round((distM / 4.16 + 90) / 60));
    const parsed = { latLngs, distM, bikeMins };
    bikeRouteGeoCache.set(key, parsed);
    return parsed;
  } catch (e) {
    clearTimeout(tid);
    return null;
  }
}

function drawWalkingLeg(targetMap, fromPt, toPt, options = {}) {
  if (!targetMap || typeof L === 'undefined' || !fromPt || !toPt) return null;
  if (!isValidRailOrFerryCoord(fromPt[0], fromPt[1]) || !isValidRailOrFerryCoord(toPt[0], toPt[1])) return null;
  const dKm = calculateDistanceKm(fromPt[0], fromPt[1], toPt[0], toPt[1]);
  if (!Number.isFinite(dKm) || dKm < 0.025) return null;

  const isBike = Boolean(options.isBike);
  const isLongWalkGuide = (!isBike && dKm > 0.40);
  const cacheKey = isBike
    ? `${fromPt[0].toFixed(4)},${fromPt[1].toFixed(4)}_${toPt[0].toFixed(4)},${toPt[1].toFixed(4)}`
    : '';
  const cachedBike = (isBike && bikeRouteGeoCache.has(cacheKey)) ? bikeRouteGeoCache.get(cacheKey) : null;

  const coords = cachedBike
    ? cachedBike.latLngs
    : (isLongWalkGuide ? buildGentleCurvePoints(fromPt, toPt, 14) : [fromPt, toPt]);

  const line = L.polyline(coords, {
    color: isBike ? '#34C759' : '#007AFF',
    weight: isBike ? 4.5 : (isLongWalkGuide ? 3 : 4),
    dashArray: isBike ? (cachedBike ? '10, 5' : '8, 6') : (isLongWalkGuide ? '2, 7' : '4, 7'),
    opacity: isLongWalkGuide ? 0.56 : 0.90,
    lineCap: 'round',
    lineJoin: 'round'
  }).addTo(targetMap);

  const isZh = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'zh');
  if (cachedBike) {
    const bikeTip = isZh
      ? `🚲 單車路線 ${cachedBike.distM}m (~${cachedBike.bikeMins}分)`
      : `🚲 Bike Route ${cachedBike.distM}m (~${cachedBike.bikeMins}m)`;
    line.bindTooltip(bikeTip, { sticky: true, className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
  } else {
    const guideNote = isLongWalkGuide
      ? (isZh ? ' (直線示意 · 按「導航 ↗」查步行路線)' : ' (Direct guide · Tap Map ↗ for walking route)')
      : '';
    const tipLabel = (options.label || '') + guideNote;
    if (tipLabel) {
      line.bindTooltip(tipLabel, { sticky: true, className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
    }
  }

  // Asynchronously upgrade straight bike fallback to real OSRM street/cycle-track geometry
  if (isBike && !cachedBike) {
    const myDrawToken = options.drawToken ?? xferDrawToken;
    fetchOsrmBikeGeometry(fromPt, toPt).then((res) => {
      if (!res || myDrawToken !== xferDrawToken || !targetMap.hasLayer(line)) return;
      line.setLatLngs(res.latLngs);
      line.setStyle({ dashArray: '10, 5', opacity: 0.94 });
      const upgradedTip = isZh
        ? `🚲 單車路線 ${res.distM}m (~${res.bikeMins}分)`
        : `🚲 Bike Route ${res.distM}m (~${res.bikeMins}m)`;
      if (line.getTooltip()) line.setTooltipContent(upgradedTip);
      else line.bindTooltip(upgradedTip, { sticky: true, className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
      fitTransferPlannerMapBounds();
    });
  }

  return line;
}

// ============================================================================
function cycleMapSheetHeight() {
  const mapEl = document.getElementById('map-container');
  if (!mapEl) return;
  detailMapHeightState = (detailMapHeightState + 1) % 3;
  mapEl.style.height = detailMapHeightState === 0 ? '35dvh' : (detailMapHeightState === 1 ? '64dvh' : '22dvh');
  setTimeout(() => window.mapInstance?.invalidateSize(false), 120);
}

function syncDetailGpsLockBtnUI() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('mapGpsRecenterBtn');
  if (btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', Boolean(isDetailGpsLocked));
}

function toggleDetailGpsLock() {
  triggerHaptic(12);
  isDetailGpsLocked = !isDetailGpsLocked;
  syncDetailGpsLockBtnUI();
  if (isDetailGpsLocked) {
    if (window.mapInstance && lastKnownPos) {
      window.mapInstance.flyTo([lastKnownPos.lat, lastKnownPos.lon], Math.min(Math.max(window.mapInstance.getZoom() || 15, 15), 16), { animate: true, duration: 0.35 });
    }
    showToast(currentLang === 'zh' ? '🔒 已鎖定 GPS 跟隨 (再按解鎖)' : '🔒 GPS Follow Locked (Tap again to unlock)');
  } else {
    showToast(currentLang === 'zh' ? '🔓 已解除 GPS 鎖定' : '🔓 GPS Follow Unlocked');
  }
}

function zoomMapIn() { window.mapInstance?.zoomIn(); }
function zoomMapOut() { window.mapInstance?.zoomOut(); }

function drawRouteOnMap(group, activeStopIdx = 0) {
  if (typeof L === 'undefined') return;
  const startLat = (typeof lastKnownPos !== 'undefined' && lastKnownPos?.lat) ? lastKnownPos.lat : 22.2855;
  const startLon = (typeof lastKnownPos !== 'undefined' && lastKnownPos?.lon) ? lastKnownPos.lon : 114.1577;
  if (!window.mapInstance) {
    window.mapInstance = L.map('map-canvas-inner', { zoomControl: false, preferCanvas: true }).setView([startLat, startLon], 13);
    window.attachStandardMapLayers(window.mapInstance);
    window.mapInstance.on('dragstart', () => {
      if (isDetailGpsLocked) {
        isDetailGpsLocked = false;
        syncDetailGpsLockBtnUI();
      }
    });
    attachMapResizeObserver('map-container', window.mapInstance);
  }
  try { window.mapInstance.invalidateSize(false); } catch (e) {}

  if (detailPolylineLayer) { try { window.mapInstance.removeLayer(detailPolylineLayer); } catch (e) {} detailPolylineLayer = null; }
  Object.values(mapMarkers).forEach(m => { try { window.mapInstance.removeLayer(m); } catch (e) {} });
  mapMarkers = {};

  const stops = getStopsForGroup(group, detailTargetStopKey);
  if (!stops.length) return;

  const isLight = document.documentElement.classList.contains('light-mode');
  const routeAccent = group?.colorHex || window.__GET_ROUTE_COLOR_HEX__(group?.mode, group?.company, group?.routeNo);
  const polyPts = stops
    .map(s => [parseFloat(s.latitude), parseFloat(s.longitude)])
    .filter(p => isValidRailOrFerryCoord(p[0], p[1]));

  currentDetailRoadCoords = polyPts;

  const lineStyle = { color: routeAccent, weight: 5, opacity: 0.92, lineJoin: 'round', lineCap: 'round' };

  if (group?.mode === 'MTR' && stops.length >= 4 && isParallelRailTerminusPair(stops[stops.length - 2]?.stopId, stops[stops.length - 1]?.stopId)) {
    const trunkStops = stops.slice(0, -2);
    const termA = stops[stops.length - 2];
    const termB = stops[stops.length - 1];
    const forkStopB = (group.routeNo === 'TKL' && stops.find(s => s.stopId === 'TKO')) || trunkStops[trunkStops.length - 1];
    const branchAPts = [...trunkStops, termA].map(s => [parseFloat(s.latitude), parseFloat(s.longitude)]).filter(p => isValidRailOrFerryCoord(p[0], p[1]));
    const branchBPts = [forkStopB, termB].map(s => [parseFloat(s.latitude), parseFloat(s.longitude)]).filter(p => isValidRailOrFerryCoord(p[0], p[1]));
    detailPolylineLayer = L.layerGroup([
      L.polyline(branchAPts, lineStyle),
      L.polyline(branchBPts, { ...lineStyle, dashArray: '6, 6' })
    ]).addTo(window.mapInstance);
  } else if (group?.mode === 'MTR' && stops.length >= 4 && isParallelRailTerminusPair(stops[0]?.stopId, stops[1]?.stopId)) {
    const termA = stops[0];
    const termB = stops[1];
    const trunkStops = stops.slice(2);
    const forkStopB = (group.routeNo === 'TKL' && stops.find(s => s.stopId === 'TKO')) || trunkStops[0];
    const branchAPts = [termA, ...trunkStops].map(s => [parseFloat(s.latitude), parseFloat(s.longitude)]).filter(p => isValidRailOrFerryCoord(p[0], p[1]));
    const branchBPts = [termB, forkStopB].map(s => [parseFloat(s.latitude), parseFloat(s.longitude)]).filter(p => isValidRailOrFerryCoord(p[0], p[1]));
    detailPolylineLayer = L.layerGroup([
      L.polyline(branchAPts, lineStyle),
      L.polyline(branchBPts, { ...lineStyle, dashArray: '6, 6' })
    ]).addTo(window.mapInstance);
  } else if (polyPts.length >= 2) {
    detailPolylineLayer = L.polyline(polyPts, lineStyle).addTo(window.mapInstance);
  }

  stops.forEach((s, idx) => {
    const lat = parseFloat(s.latitude), lon = parseFloat(s.longitude);
    if (!isValidRailOrFerryCoord(lat, lon)) return;
    const isAct = (idx === activeStopIdx);
    const bgCol = isAct ? routeAccent : (isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#2C2C2E');
    const fgCol = isAct ? '#FFFFFF' : (isLight ? '#1C1C1E' : '#FFFFFF');
    const bdrCol = isAct ? '#FFFFFF' : routeAccent;
    const m = L.marker([lat, lon], {
      icon: L.divIcon({
        className: 'custom-stop-icon',
        html: `<div style="width:${isAct ? 22 : 16}px;height:${isAct ? 22 : 16}px;background:${bgCol};color:${fgCol};border:2.5px solid ${bdrCol};border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:0.56rem;font-weight:800;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);">${idx + 1}</div>`,
        iconSize: [22, 22], iconAnchor: [11, 11]
      }),
      zIndexOffset: isAct ? 600 : 200
    }).addTo(window.mapInstance);
    m.bindTooltip(`${idx + 1}. ${getStopName(s)}`, { direction: 'top', offset: [0, -8], className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
    m.on('click', () => expandStop(idx, false, false, false));
    mapMarkers[idx] = m;
  });

  isDetailGpsLocked = false;
  syncDetailGpsLockBtnUI();
  updateUserMarkers();

  if (polyPts.length >= 2) {
    try {
      window.mapInstance.invalidateSize(false);
      window.mapInstance.fitBounds(L.latLngBounds(polyPts), { paddingTopLeft: [36, 36], paddingBottomRight: [36, 44], maxZoom: 16 });
    } catch (e) {}
  }
}

function highlightActiveStopMarkerOnMap(activeIdx) {
  if (!window.mapInstance || !detailRouteGroup) return;
  const stops = getStopsForGroup(detailRouteGroup, detailTargetStopKey);
  const isLight = document.documentElement.classList.contains('light-mode');
  const routeAccent = detailRouteGroup?.colorHex || window.__GET_ROUTE_COLOR_HEX__(detailRouteGroup?.mode, detailRouteGroup?.company, detailRouteGroup?.routeNo);
  Object.entries(mapMarkers).forEach(([idxStr, m]) => {
    const idx = Number(idxStr);
    const isAct = (idx === activeIdx);
    const bgCol = isAct ? routeAccent : (isLight ? '#FFFFFF' : '#2C2C2E');
    const fgCol = isAct ? '#FFFFFF' : (isLight ? '#1C1C1E' : '#FFFFFF');
    const bdrCol = isAct ? '#FFFFFF' : routeAccent;
    m.setIcon(L.divIcon({
      className: 'custom-stop-icon',
      html: `<div style="width:${isAct ? 22 : 16}px;height:${isAct ? 22 : 16}px;background:${bgCol};color:${fgCol};border:2.5px solid ${bdrCol};border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:0.56rem;font-weight:800;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);">${idx + 1}</div>`,
      iconSize: [22, 22], iconAnchor: [11, 11]
    }));
    if (isAct && stops[idx]) m.openTooltip();
  });
}

// ============================================================================
// 4. STOP BROWSER MAP (window.browseMapInstance)
// ============================================================================
function getStopImportanceTier(s) {
  if (!s) return 0;
  if (s.mode === 'MTR' && s.company !== 'LRT' && !s.lrtCode) return 4000;
  if (s.mode === 'FERRY') return 3500;
  if (s.mode === 'MTR' && (s.company === 'LRT' || s.lrtCode)) return 2500;
  return (s.routeNos ? s.routeNos.size : 1);
}

function rebuildBrowseMapSpatialGrid() {
  browseMapStopGrid.clear();
  browseMapExitGrid.clear();

  const seenCellDedup = new Set();
  for (let i = 0; i < allStops.length; i++) {
    const s = allStops[i];
    const lat = parseFloat(s.latitude), lon = parseFloat(s.longitude);
    if (!isValidRailOrFerryCoord(lat, lon)) continue;
    const isLrt = (s.mode === 'MTR' && (s.company === 'LRT' || s.lrtCode));
    const subType = s.mode === 'FERRY' ? 'FERRY' : (isLrt ? 'LRT' : (s.mode === 'MTR' ? 'MTR' : 'BUS'));
    const dedupSig = subType === 'MTR'
      ? `MTR_${String(s.mtrCode || s.stopId || getBaseStopNameKey(s.stopName_e)).toUpperCase()}`
      : (subType === 'LRT'
        ? `LRT_${String(s.lrtCode || s.stopId || getBaseStopNameKey(s.stopName_e)).toUpperCase()}`
        : (subType === 'BUS'
          ? `BUS_${getBaseStopNameKey(s.stopName_e)}_${lat.toFixed(4)}_${lon.toFixed(4)}`
          : `${subType}_${normStationLookupKey(s.stopName_e) || getBaseStopNameKey(s.stopName_e)}`));
    if (seenCellDedup.has(dedupSig)) continue;
    seenCellDedup.add(dedupSig);

    const ck = `${Math.floor(lat * 100)}_${Math.floor(lon * 100)}`;
    if (!browseMapStopGrid.has(ck)) browseMapStopGrid.set(ck, []);
    browseMapStopGrid.get(ck).push(s);
  }

  for (const bucket of browseMapStopGrid.values()) {
    bucket.sort((a, b) => getStopImportanceTier(b) - getStopImportanceTier(a));
  }
}

function buildBrowseMapActionPopupHtml({ stopKey = '', nameEn, nameTc, routesText = '', lat, lon, nodeType = 'BUS', hasStopRadar = false }) {
  const isZh = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'zh');
  const dispName = escapeHtml(isZh && nameTc ? nameTc : nameEn);
  const escK = escapeJsAttr(stopKey);
  const escE = escapeJsAttr(nameEn);
  const escZ = escapeJsAttr(nameTc || nameEn);

  const subLine = routesText
    ? `<div style="font-size:0.72rem; opacity:0.8; margin:2px 0 8px;">${escapeHtml(routesText)}</div>`
    : `<div style="font-size:0.70rem; opacity:0.75; margin:2px 0 8px;">${Number(lat).toFixed(5)}, ${Number(lon).toFixed(5)}</div>`;

  const radarBtnHtml = hasStopRadar
    ? `<button onclick="window.browseMapInstance?.closePopup(); window.xferMapInstance?.closePopup(); selectStopFromSearch('${escK}'); switchView('main', false);"
         style="width:100%; margin-top:5px; padding:6px 10px; border-radius:8px; border:1px solid #007AFF; background:#fff; color:#007AFF; font-weight:700; font-size:0.76rem; cursor:pointer;">
         ${isZh ? '查看此站實時班次' : 'View Stop Live ETAs'}
       </button>`
    : '';

  return `
    <div style="min-width:195px; max-width:240px; font-family:inherit; color:#1C1C1E;">
      <div style="font-weight:800; font-size:0.84rem; line-height:1.3;">${dispName}</div>
      ${subLine}
      <div style="display:flex; gap:6px;">
        <button onclick="sendMapPointToTransferPlanner('home', '${escK}', '${escE}', '${escZ}', ${lat}, ${lon}, '${nodeType}')"
          style="flex:1; padding:6px 8px; border-radius:8px; border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1; background:#fff; color:#1C1C1E; font-weight:800; font-size:0.75rem; cursor:pointer;">
          ${isZh ? '設為起點' : 'From Here'}
        </button>
        <button onclick="sendMapPointToTransferPlanner('work', '${escK}', '${escE}', '${escZ}', ${lat}, ${lon}, '${nodeType}')"
          style="flex:1; padding:6px 8px; border-radius:8px; border:none; background:#007AFF; color:#fff; font-weight:800; font-size:0.75rem; cursor:pointer;">
          ${isZh ? '去這裡' : 'Go Here'}
        </button>
      </div>
      ${radarBtnHtml}
    </div>
  `;
}

function scheduleBrowseMapPinDiff() {
  clearTimeout(browseMapMoveDebounceTimer);
  browseMapMoveDebounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    renderBrowseMapVisiblePins();
  }, 80);
}

function clearAllActiveBrowseMarkers() {
  if (!window.browseMapInstance) return;
  for (const marker of activeBrowseMarkersByKey.values()) {
    try { window.browseMapInstance.removeLayer(marker); } catch (e) {}
  }
  activeBrowseMarkersByKey.clear();
}

function initAndRenderBrowseMap() {
  if (typeof L === 'undefined') return;
  const centerLat = lastKnownPos?.lat || 22.2855;
  const centerLon = lastKnownPos?.lon || 114.1577;
  if (!window.browseMapInstance) {
    window.browseMapInstance = L.map('browse-map-canvas', { zoomControl: false, preferCanvas: true }).setView([centerLat, centerLon], 15);
    window.attachStandardMapLayers(window.browseMapInstance);
    window.browseMapInstance.on('moveend', scheduleBrowseMapPinDiff);
    attachMapResizeObserver('browse-map-canvas', window.browseMapInstance, () => renderBrowseMapVisiblePins());

    window.browseMapInstance.on('contextmenu', (e) => {
      const lat = Number(e.latlng.lat.toFixed(5));
      const lon = Number(e.latlng.lng.toFixed(5));
      triggerHaptic(14);
      if (browseMapClickedSpotMarker) {
        try { window.browseMapInstance.removeLayer(browseMapClickedSpotMarker); } catch (err) {}
      }
      const distTag = getStopDistrictTag({ stopName_e: '', latitude: lat, longitude: lon });
      const labelEn = distTag ? `Pinned Spot (${distTag})` : `Pinned Map Spot`;
      const labelZh = distTag ? `地圖標記點 (${distTag})` : `地圖標記點`;

      browseMapClickedSpotMarker = L.circleMarker([lat, lon], {
        radius: 8, fillColor: '#007AFF', color: '#FFFFFF', weight: 2.5, fillOpacity: 0.95
      }).addTo(window.browseMapInstance);

      const popupHtml = buildBrowseMapActionPopupHtml({
        stopKey: `MAP_PIN_${lat}_${lon}`,
        nameEn: labelEn,
        nameTc: labelZh,
        lat,
        lon,
        nodeType: 'ADDRESS',
        hasStopRadar: false
      });
      browseMapClickedSpotMarker.bindPopup(popupHtml, { closeButton: true, offset: [0, -4] }).openPopup();
    });

    if (!window.browseMapInstance._compassBound) {
      window.browseMapInstance._compassBound = true;
      autoStartCompassCone(false);
      window.browseMapInstance.once('click dragstart', () => autoStartCompassCone(true));
    }
  } else {
    window.browseMapInstance.invalidateSize(false);
  }
  updateUserMarkers();
  if (!browseMapStopGrid.size && allStops.length) rebuildBrowseMapSpatialGrid();
  renderBrowseMapVisiblePins();
}

function createBrowseMapStopMarker(s, displayLat = null, displayLon = null) {
  const trueLat = parseFloat(s.latitude), trueLon = parseFloat(s.longitude);
  const lat = displayLat ?? trueLat;
  const lon = displayLon ?? trueLon;
  const isLrt = (s.mode === 'MTR' && (s.company === 'LRT' || s.lrtCode));
  const subType = s.mode === 'FERRY' ? 'FERRY' : (isLrt ? 'LRT' : (s.mode === 'MTR' ? 'MTR' : 'BUS'));
  const routesStr = Array.from(s.routeNos || []).map(r => cleanFerryLabel(r)).slice(0, 8).join(', ');

  let m;
  if (subType !== 'BUS') {
    const tagTxt = subType === 'MTR' ? 'MTR' : (subType === 'LRT' ? 'LR' : 'FER');
    const bgCol = subType === 'MTR' ? '#007AFF' : (subType === 'LRT' ? '#5856D6' : '#0090D9');
    const sz = subType === 'LRT' ? 20 : 24;
    m = L.marker([lat, lon], {
      icon: L.divIcon({
        className: '',
        html: `<div style="width:${sz}px;height:${sz}px;border-radius:6px;background:${bgCol};color:#fff;border:2px solid #fff;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:0.54rem;font-weight:800;box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);">${tagTxt}</div>`,
        iconSize: [sz, sz],
        iconAnchor: [sz / 2, sz / 2]
      }),
      zIndexOffset: subType === 'MTR' ? 800 : 700
    }).addTo(window.browseMapInstance);
  } else {
    m = L.circleMarker([lat, lon], {
      radius: 6,
      fillColor: '#FFFFFF',
      color: '#FF3B30',
      weight: 2.2,
      fillOpacity: 0.95
    }).addTo(window.browseMapInstance);
  }

  m.bindTooltip(`${getStopName(s)}${routesStr ? ` (${routesStr})` : ''}`, { direction: 'top', className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
  m.bindPopup(buildBrowseMapActionPopupHtml({
    stopKey: s.stopKey,
    nameEn: cleanFerryLabel(s.stopName_e),
    nameTc: cleanFerryLabel(s.name_tc || s.stopName_e),
    routesText: routesStr,
    lat: trueLat,
    lon: trueLon,
    nodeType: s.mode || 'BUS',
    hasStopRadar: true
  }), { closeButton: true, offset: [0, -4] });
  return m;
}

function renderBrowseMapVisiblePins() {
  if (!window.browseMapInstance) return;
  if (!browseMapStopGrid.size && allStops.length) rebuildBrowseMapSpatialGrid();
  updateUserMarkers();

  const bounds = window.browseMapInstance.getBounds();
  const zoom = window.browseMapInstance.getZoom() || 15;
  const center = window.browseMapInstance.getCenter();
  const cLatCell = center.lat * 100, cLonCell = center.lng * 100;

  const minLa = Math.floor(bounds.getSouth() * 100) - 1;
  const maxLa = Math.floor(bounds.getNorth() * 100) + 1;
  const minLo = Math.floor(bounds.getWest() * 100) - 1;
  const maxLo = Math.floor(bounds.getEast() * 100) + 1;

  const visibleCells = [];
  for (let la = minLa; la <= maxLa; la++) {
    for (let lo = minLo; lo <= maxLo; lo++) {
      const ck = `${la}_${lo}`;
      if (browseMapStopGrid.has(ck)) {
        const distSq = (la + 0.5 - cLatCell) ** 2 + (lo + 0.5 - cLonCell) ** 2;
        visibleCells.push({ ck, distSq });
      }
    }
  }
  visibleCells.sort((a, b) => a.distSq - b.distSq);

  const desiredPins = new Map();
  const railAndFerryHubs = [];
  const maxTotalPins = zoom >= 16 ? 340 : 260;

  for (const { ck } of visibleCells) {
    const cellStops = browseMapStopGrid.get(ck) || [];
    for (const s of cellStops) {
      const isHeavyMtr = (s.mode === 'MTR' && s.company !== 'LRT' && !s.lrtCode);
      if (!isHeavyMtr && s.mode !== 'FERRY') continue;
      const lat = parseFloat(s.latitude), lon = parseFloat(s.longitude);
      if (bounds.contains([lat, lon])) {
        desiredPins.set(s.stopKey, { kind: 'STOP', item: s, dispLat: lat, dispLon: lon });
        railAndFerryHubs.push([lat, lon]);
      }
    }
  }

  const perCellBusQuota = zoom <= 13 ? 3 : (zoom <= 15 ? 14 : 999);
  for (const { ck } of visibleCells) {
    if (desiredPins.size >= maxTotalPins) break;
    const cellStops = browseMapStopGrid.get(ck) || [];
    let busInCell = 0;

    for (const s of cellStops) {
      if (desiredPins.size >= maxTotalPins) break;
      if (desiredPins.has(s.stopKey)) continue;
      const lat = parseFloat(s.latitude), lon = parseFloat(s.longitude);
      if (!bounds.contains([lat, lon])) continue;

      const isLrt = (s.mode === 'MTR' && (s.company === 'LRT' || s.lrtCode));
      if (isLrt) {
        if (zoom >= 13) {
          desiredPins.set(s.stopKey, { kind: 'STOP', item: s, dispLat: lat, dispLon: lon });
          railAndFerryHubs.push([lat, lon]);
        }
        continue;
      }

      const routeCount = s.routeNos ? s.routeNos.size : 1;
      if (zoom <= 13 && routeCount < 6) continue;
      if (busInCell >= perCellBusQuota) continue;
      busInCell++;

      let dispLat = lat, dispLon = lon;
      for (const [rLat, rLon] of railAndFerryHubs) {
        if (Math.abs(lat - rLat) < 0.00022 && Math.abs(lon - rLon) < 0.00022) {
          const angle = ((s.stopKey.charCodeAt(0) * 37 + s.stopKey.charCodeAt(s.stopKey.length - 1) + busInCell * 3) % 8) * (Math.PI / 4);
          dispLat = rLat + Math.sin(angle) * 0.00020;
          dispLon = rLon + Math.cos(angle) * 0.00022;
          break;
        }
      }
      desiredPins.set(s.stopKey, { kind: 'STOP', item: s, dispLat, dispLon });
    }
  }

  for (const [key, marker] of activeBrowseMarkersByKey.entries()) {
    if (!desiredPins.has(key)) {
      if (typeof marker.isPopupOpen === 'function' && marker.isPopupOpen()) continue;
      try { window.browseMapInstance.removeLayer(marker); } catch (e) {}
      activeBrowseMarkersByKey.delete(key);
    }
  }

  for (const [key, spec] of desiredPins.entries()) {
    if (activeBrowseMarkersByKey.has(key)) continue;
    const marker = createBrowseMapStopMarker(spec.item, spec.dispLat, spec.dispLon);
    activeBrowseMarkersByKey.set(key, marker);
  }
}

async function recenterBrowseMapToGps(forceFreshFix = false) {
  triggerHaptic(12);
  autoStartCompassCone(true);
  if (forceFreshFix) {
    showToast(currentLang === 'zh' ? '🎯 正在更新 GPS 定位...' : '🎯 Acquiring live GPS fix...');
    await refreshGPS(3200, true);
  }
  updateUserMarkers();
  if (window.browseMapInstance && lastKnownPos) {
    window.browseMapInstance.flyTo([lastKnownPos.lat, lastKnownPos.lon], Math.max(window.browseMapInstance.getZoom() || 15, 16), { duration: 0.4 });
    setTimeout(() => userMarkerBrowse?.openTooltip(), 420);
  }
}

// ============================================================================
// 5. TRANSFER PLANNER MAP (window.xferMapInstance)
// ============================================================================
function setXferMapHeightPx(px, expanded = false) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('xferMapWrapper');
  const btn = document.getElementById('xferMapExpandBtn');
  if (!wrap) return;
  isXferMapExpanded = expanded;
  wrap.style.height = `${px}px`;
  if (btn) {
    const isZh = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'zh');
    btn.innerText = expanded ? (isZh ? '⬌ 收起地圖' : '⬌ Collapse Map') : (isZh ? '⬍ 展開地圖' : '⬍ Full Map');
  }
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (window.xferMapInstance) {
      window.xferMapInstance.invalidateSize(false);
      fitTransferPlannerMapBounds();
    }
  }, 200);
}

function toggleXferMapFullScreen() {
  triggerHaptic(10);
  if (isXferMapExpanded) setXferMapHeightPx(220, false);
  else setXferMapHeightPx(Math.round(window.innerHeight * 0.62), true);
}

function initXferMapDragResize() {
  const handle = document.getElementById('xferMapDragHandle');
  const wrap = document.getElementById('xferMapWrapper');
  if (!handle || !wrap || handle._dragBound) return;
  handle._dragBound = true;
  let startY = 0, startH = 220, dragging = false;
  const onStart = (y) => { dragging = true; startY = y; startH = wrap.offsetHeight; wrap.style.transition = 'none'; };
  const onMove = (y) => {
    if (!dragging) return;
    const newH = Math.max(140, Math.min(Math.round(window.innerHeight * 0.72), startH + (y - startY)));
    wrap.style.height = `${newH}px`;
    window.xferMapInstance?.invalidateSize(false);
  };
  const onEnd = () => {
    if (!dragging) return;
    dragging = false;
    wrap.style.transition = 'height 0.25s ease';
    isXferMapExpanded = wrap.offsetHeight > 320;
    window.xferMapInstance?.invalidateSize(false);
  };
  handle.addEventListener('touchstart', e => { if (e.touches[0]) onStart(e.touches[0].clientY); }, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('touchmove', e => { if (dragging && e.touches[0]) onMove(e.touches[0].clientY); }, { passive: true });
  window.addEventListener('touchend', onEnd);
  handle.addEventListener('mousedown', e => onStart(e.clientY));
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', e => onMove(e.clientY));
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', onEnd);
}

if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initXferMapDragResize);
} else {
  initXferMapDragResize();
}

function ensureXferMapInitialized() {
  if (typeof L === 'undefined') return null;
  initXferMapDragResize();
  if (!window.xferMapInstance) {
    const startLat = (typeof lastKnownPos !== 'undefined' && lastKnownPos?.lat) ? lastKnownPos.lat : 22.30;
    const startLon = (typeof lastKnownPos !== 'undefined' && lastKnownPos?.lon) ? lastKnownPos.lon : 114.12;
    window.xferMapInstance = L.map('xfer-map-canvas', { zoomControl: false, preferCanvas: true }).setView([startLat, startLon], 12);
    window.attachStandardMapLayers(window.xferMapInstance);
    attachMapResizeObserver('xferMapWrapper', window.xferMapInstance, () => fitTransferPlannerMapBounds());
  }
  return window.xferMapInstance;
}

function fitTransferPlannerMapBounds() {
  const map = window.xferMapInstance;
  if (!map || !xferMapLayers.length) return;
  const wrap = document.getElementById('xferMapWrapper');
  if (wrap && (wrap.offsetWidth < 40 || wrap.offsetHeight < 40)) {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => fitTransferPlannerMapBounds());
    return;
  }
  try { map.invalidateSize(false); } catch (e) {}

  const pts = [];
  xferMapLayers.forEach(l => {
    if (typeof l.getLatLng === 'function') pts.push(l.getLatLng());
    else if (typeof l.getLatLngs === 'function') pts.push(...l.getLatLngs().flat(2));
  });
  if (pts.length >= 2) {
    try {
      map.fitBounds(L.latLngBounds(pts), {
        paddingTopLeft: [50, 56],
        paddingBottomRight: [50, 58],
        maxZoom: 16,
        animate: false
      });
    } catch (e) {}
  }
}

function drawTransferCorridorOnMap(spec, selectedCard) {
  const map = ensureXferMapInitialized();
  if (!map) return;

  try { map.invalidateSize(false); } catch (e) {}

  const myDrawToken = ++xferDrawToken;

  xferMapLayers.forEach(l => { try { map.removeLayer(l); } catch (e) {} });
  xferMapLayers = [];

  if (!spec?.fromMeta || !spec?.toMeta) return;
  const isZh = (typeof currentLang !== 'undefined' && currentLang === 'zh');

  // Anchor Origin/Dest downward ('down') and Interchange pins upward ('up') to prevent label collision at low zoom
  const addPin = (lat, lon, label, bgCol, fgCol = '#FFFFFF', zOff = 700, placement = 'up') => {
    if (!isValidRailOrFerryCoord(lat, lon)) return;
    const isDown = (placement === 'down');
    const pinTransform = isDown ? 'translate(-50%, 30%)' : 'translate(-50%, -125%)';
    const dotHtml = `<div style="position:absolute;left:0;top:0;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:${bgCol};border:2px solid #FFFFFF;box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.4);"></div>`;
    const pillHtml = `<div style="position:absolute;left:0;top:0;transform:${pinTransform};display:inline-block;max-width:155px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;background:${bgCol};color:${fgCol};border:2px solid #FFFFFF;font-size:0.62rem;font-weight:800;white-space:nowrap;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.34);pointer-events:auto;">${escapeHtml(label)}</div>`;
    const m = L.marker([lat, lon], {
      icon: L.divIcon({
        className: '',
        html: `<div style="position:relative;width:0;height:0;">${dotHtml}${pillHtml}</div>`,
        iconSize: [0, 0],
        iconAnchor: [0, 0]
      }),
      zIndexOffset: zOff
    }).addTo(map);
    m.bindTooltip(label, {
      direction: isDown ? 'bottom' : 'top',
      offset: isDown ? [0, 24] : [0, -24],
      className: 'kmb-map-stop-label'
    });
    xferMapLayers.push(m);
  };

  const drawTransitLeg = (group, bStop, aStop, fallbackColor = '#007AFF') => {
    const stopPts = sliceRouteStopPoints(group, bStop, aStop);
    const pts = stopPts.map(x => x.pt);
    const col = group?.colorHex || window.__GET_ROUTE_COLOR_HEX__(group?.mode, group?.company, group?.routeNo) || fallbackColor;
    if (pts.length >= 2) {
      const poly = L.polyline(pts, {
        color: col,
        weight: 5,
        opacity: 0.92,
        lineJoin: 'round',
        lineCap: 'round'
      }).addTo(map);
      const badge = window.getDisplayRouteBadge ? window.getDisplayRouteBadge(group) : (group?.routeNo || '');
      if (badge) poly.bindTooltip(`${badge}`, { sticky: true, className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
      xferMapLayers.push(poly);

      stopPts.forEach((entry, idx) => {
        const isEndpoint = (idx === 0 || idx === stopPts.length - 1);
        const dot = L.circleMarker(entry.pt, {
          radius: isEndpoint ? 5.2 : 3.6,
          fillColor: '#FFFFFF',
          color: col,
          weight: isEndpoint ? 2.6 : 2.0,
          fillOpacity: 1
        }).addTo(map);
        if (entry.stop) {
          dot.bindTooltip(getStopName(entry.stop), { direction: 'top', offset: [0, -6], className: 'kmb-map-stop-label' });
        }
        xferMapLayers.push(dot);
      });
    }
    return pts;
  };

  const originPt = [spec.fromMeta.lat, spec.fromMeta.lon];
  const destPt = [spec.toMeta.lat, spec.toMeta.lon];
  addPin(originPt[0], originPt[1], isZh ? '起點' : 'Origin', '#007AFF', '#FFFFFF', 820, 'down');
  addPin(destPt[0], destPt[1], isZh ? '終點' : 'Dest', '#1C1C1E', '#FFFFFF', 820, 'down');

  const pushWalk = (pA, pB, isBike = false, label = '') => {
    const wl = drawWalkingLeg(map, pA, pB, { isBike, label, drawToken: myDrawToken });
    if (wl) xferMapLayers.push(wl);
  };

  if (selectedCard?.type === 'WALK_BIKE') {
    const wb = selectedCard.item;
    pushWalk(originPt, destPt, wb.isBike, `${wb.isBike ? '🚲' : '🚶'} ${wb.distM}m (~${wb.totalMins}m)`);
  } else if (selectedCard?.type === 'DIRECT') {
    const dp = selectedCard.item;
    const pts = drawTransitLeg(dp.group, dp.boardStopObj, dp.alightStopObj, '#007AFF');
    const boardPt = getStopLatLon(dp.accessExitObj) || pts[0] || getStopLatLon(dp.boardStopObj);
    const alightPt = getStopLatLon(dp.egressExitObj) || pts[pts.length - 1] || getStopLatLon(dp.alightStopObj);

    pushWalk(originPt, boardPt, dp.accessByBike, dp.accessWalkDistM ? `${dp.accessByBike ? '🚲' : '🚶'} ${dp.accessWalkDistM}m` : '');
    pushWalk(alightPt, destPt, dp.egressByBike, dp.egressWalkDistM ? `${dp.egressByBike ? '🚲' : '🚶'} ${dp.egressWalkDistM}m` : '');
  } else if (selectedCard?.type === 'XFER') {
    const cor = selectedCard.item;
    const node = cor.xferStopNodes?.[activeXferStopNodeIdx] || cor.xferStopNodes?.[0];
    if (node) {
      const p1 = drawTransitLeg(cor.leg1Group, cor.board1StopObj, node.alight1StopObj, '#007AFF');
      const firstBoardPt = getStopLatLon(cor.accessExitObj) || p1[0] || getStopLatLon(cor.board1StopObj);
      pushWalk(originPt, firstBoardPt, cor.accessByBike, cor.accessWalkDistM ? `${cor.accessByBike ? '🚲' : '🚶'} ${cor.accessWalkDistM}m` : '');

      const a1Pt = p1[p1.length - 1] || getStopLatLon(node.alight1StopObj);
      const b2Pt = getStopLatLon(node.board2StopObj);
      pushWalk(a1Pt, b2Pt, false, node.walkDistM ? `🚶 ${node.walkDistM}m` : '');
      addPin(node.lat, node.lon, `⇄ ${getStopName(node.board2StopObj)}`, '#FF9500', '#FFFFFF', 760, 'up');

      const leg2EndObj = cor.leg3Options?.length ? cor.leg3Options[0].prevAlightStopObj : cor.alight2StopObj;
      const p2 = drawTransitLeg(cor.leg2Group, node.board2StopObj, leg2EndObj, '#34C759');

      let lastTransitEndPt = p2[p2.length - 1] || getStopLatLon(leg2EndObj);

      (cor.leg3Options || []).forEach(l3 => {
        const prevPt = lastTransitEndPt || getStopLatLon(l3.prevAlightStopObj);
        const b3Pt = getStopLatLon(l3.boardStopObj);
        pushWalk(prevPt, b3Pt, false, l3.walkDistM ? `🚶 ${l3.walkDistM}m` : '');
        addPin(l3.boardStopObj.lat, l3.boardStopObj.lon, `⇄ ${getStopName(l3.boardStopObj)}`, '#5856D6', '#FFFFFF', 750, 'up');

        const p3 = drawTransitLeg(l3.group, l3.boardStopObj, l3.alightStopObj, '#5856D6');
        lastTransitEndPt = p3[p3.length - 1] || getStopLatLon(l3.alightStopObj);
      });

      const finalEgressStartPt = getStopLatLon(cor.egressExitObj) || lastTransitEndPt || getStopLatLon(cor.alight2StopObj);
      pushWalk(finalEgressStartPt, destPt, cor.egressByBike, cor.egressWalkDistM ? `${cor.egressByBike ? '🚲' : '🚶'} ${cor.egressWalkDistM}m` : '');
    }
  }

  fitTransferPlannerMapBounds();
}
```

// ============================================================================
// File: planner.js (johntjz/Hk-Fleet — Multi-Modal Behavioral Routing Engine)
// Patch: 2026-11-20 21:50 HKT — Best-Stop Alighting Solver, 1.35x Bike Physics, 3D BUS<->MTR Friction & LoadFactor

var userCommuteProfile = { home: null, work: null };
try {
  const savedCommute = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('transit_commute_profile_v1') || 'null');
  if (savedCommute && typeof savedCommute === 'object') userCommuteProfile = savedCommute;
} catch (e) {}

var activeCommutePickerSlot = 'home'; // 'home' | 'work' | 'hub_home' | 'hub_work'
var lastXferSolvedSpec = null;
var activeXferCorridorIdx = 0;
var activeXferStopNodeIdx = 0;

try {
